--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{Cot}\left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math]
D. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\cot \left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)=\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
[Image]

Question #3 (ID: 443035)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2(\sin \theta \cos \theta+\sqrt{3})=\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+4 \sin \theta \text { and } 0<\theta<360^{\circ} \text { then, } \theta=\text { ? }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]30^{\circ}, 150^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]90^{\circ}, 0^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]60^{\circ}, 120^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]45^{\circ}, 135^{\circ}[/math]
E. [math]\text { None }[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]2(\sin \theta \cos \theta+\sqrt{3})=\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+4 \sin \theta ; \theta=60^{\circ}[/math]
 ও [math]120^{\circ}[/math]
 • উভয়ের জন্য [math]\text { L.H.S. = R.H.S. }[/math]
 হয়

Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta+\sec \theta=-1,0<\theta<2 \pi[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত হবে -

Options:
A. [math]\pi[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta+\sec \theta=-1 \quad \theta=\pi[/math]
 হলে [math]\tan 2 \pi+\sec \pi=-1[/math]

Question #5 (ID: 443037)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology, Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান -

Options:
A. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta=1 \Rightarrow \sin 45^{\circ} \sin \theta+\cos 45^{\circ} \cos \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-45^{\circ}\right)=\cos 0 \Rightarrow \theta-45^{\circ}=0 \quad \therefore \theta=45^{\circ} \text {. }[/math]
সমাধান: [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{x}{y}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y} ; \sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y}=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}=\frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #6 (ID: 443038)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1}(\cos x)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}+x[/math]
খ. [math]x-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}-x[/math]
ঘ. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1}(\cos x)=\sin ^{-1}\left(\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-x\right)\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}-x[/math]

Explanation:

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{1+x^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]1+x^{2}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]1-x^{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\left.\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)\right\}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\left.\Rightarrow \cos \cos ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)\right\}^{2}=1-x^{2}[/math]

[Image]

Question #9 (ID: 443024)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot ^{-1} 3+\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]-1[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\left.\left.\Rightarrow x y=\sqrt{1-x^{2}-y^{2}+x^{2} y^{2}} \Rightarrow 1-\right) y^{2}+y^{2}\right)=x^{2} y^{2}-x^{2} y^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #10 (ID: 443025)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Pabna University of Science and Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{7}{11}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\pi[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{7}{11}+\frac{1}{7}+\frac{1}{13}-\frac{7}{11} \cdot \frac{1}{7} \cdot \frac{1}{13}}{1-\frac{7}{11} \cdot \frac{1}{7}-\frac{1}{7} \cdot \frac{1}{13}-\frac{1}{13} \cdot \frac{7}{11}}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{85}{85}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #12 (ID: 443027)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos A+\sin A=\sqrt{2} \cos A[/math] হলে [math]\cos A-\sin A=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \sin A[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt{2} \sin A[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{(2 \sin A)}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{3} \sin A[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos ^{2} A+\sin ^{2} A+2 \cos A \sin A=2 \cos ^{2} A \Rightarrow \cos ^{2} A-\sin ^{2} A=2 \cos A \sin A[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos A-\sin A=\frac{2 \cos A \sin A}{\sqrt{2} \cos A}=\sqrt{2} \sin A[/math]


Explanation:

Question #13 (ID: 443028)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি , [math]\cos x+\cos y=\frac{1}{5}[/math] এবং [math]\sin x+\sin y=\frac{1}{4}[/math] হয় তবে [math]\tan \left(\frac{x+y}{2}\right)=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{7}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\cos x+\cos y=\frac{1}{5} \Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{x+y}{2} \cos \frac{x-y}{2}=\frac{1}{5} \ldots .(i)[/math]
আবার [math]\sin x+\sin y=\frac{1}{4} \Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{x+y}{2} \cos \frac{x-y}{2}=\frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [math]\text { (ii) } /(\mathrm{i}) \Rightarrow \tan \frac{\mathrm{x}+\mathrm{y}}{2}=\frac{5}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #14 (ID: 443029)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2,\left(0^{\circ}<\theta<360^{\circ}\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]90^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]120^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2 \Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=1 \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos 0^{\circ} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}=60^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #15 (ID: 443030)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta=1[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math](4 n+1) \pi / 2[/math]
B. [math]-(1-4 n) \pi / 2[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
D. [math]-(4 n-1) \pi / 2[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta=1=\sin \frac{\pi}{2} \quad \therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question #16 (ID: 443031)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} x[/math] এর মান -

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\pi[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{x+\frac{1}{x}}{1-x \cdot \frac{1}{x}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{0}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #18 (ID: 443021)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Chattogram Veterinary and Animal Sciences University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec}\left(\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]0[/math]
C. [math]-1[/math]
D. [math]x[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec}\left(\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{\mathrm{x}}\right. \\\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}+\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}}{1-1}=\operatorname{cosec} \tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{2}=\operatorname{cosec} \frac{\pi}{2}=1\end{array}[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x=[/math]কত?

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{\frac{2+x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}}[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1+x}{1-x}}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1+x}{1+2 x}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathrm{x}^{2}+1}}=\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{x}^{2}+1}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{x}^{2}+2}}[/math]

Question #21 (ID: 443011)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 8+\tan ^{-1} \frac{9}{7}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{8+\frac{9}{7}}{1-8 \times \frac{9}{7}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{65}{-65}=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]

Question #23 (ID: 443013)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} x=2[/math] এবং [math]\cot x=-\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
A. [math]\operatorname{Sin} x=-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\sec x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\tan x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\operatorname{Cos} x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
E. (C,D)


Correct Answer: E.(C,D)

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} x=2 ; \cot x=-\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{\cos x}{\sin x}=-\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \cos x \operatorname{cosec} x=-\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \cos x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
আবার, [math]\tan x=\frac{\sin x}{\cos x}=\frac{1}{\cos x \operatorname{cosec} x}=\frac{1}{\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2} \cdot 2}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3} \text { So, (c), (d) both are Ans. }[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #24 (ID: 443014)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Chittagong (Unit A), University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #25 (ID: 443015)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology, Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{a}}{1+\mathrm{a}^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{b}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{b}^{2}}[/math] সমীকরণে x এর মান হল-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 a b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} x=2 \tan ^{-1} a+2 \tan ^{-1} b=2\left(\tan ^{-1} a+\tan ^{-1} b\right) \text \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{a+b}{1-a b} \quad \therefore x=\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #30 (ID: 443020)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}\right)-\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}\right)=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math] হয় তবে [math]x=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{a-b}{a+b}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
C. [math]a+b[/math]
D. [math]a-b[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}\right)-\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}\right)=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} a-2 \tan ^{-1} b=2 \tan ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} a-\tan ^{-1} b=\tan ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{a-b}{1+a b}=\tan ^{-1} x \therefore x=\frac{a-b}{1+a b}\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)=[/math] কত ?

Question #33 (ID: 443004)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2(\cos x+\sec x)=5[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]2(\cos x+\sec x)=5 \text { Or, } 2\left(\cos ^{2} x+1\right)=5 \cos x \text { Or, } 2 \cos ^{2} x-5 \cos x+2=0[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } 2 \cos ^{2} x-4 \cos x-\cos x+2=0 \text { Or, } 2 \cos x(\cos x-2)-1(\cos x-2)=0[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, }(2 \cos x-1)(\cos x-2)=0 ; \cos x=\frac{1}{2} ; x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #37 (ID: 443008)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board, Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos x+\sqrt{3} \sin x=\sqrt{2}[/math] এর সমাধান কর?

Options:
A. [math](24 n+7) \frac{\pi}{12},(24 n-1) \frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
B. [math](15 n+7) \frac{\pi}{12},(24 n+1) \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
C. [math](24 n+7) \frac{\pi}{12},(24 n-1) \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
D. [math](24 n+7) \frac{\pi}{12},(24 n+1) \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]3 \sin ^{2} x=\sqrt{2}-2 \sqrt{2} \cos x+\cos ^{2} x \Rightarrow 2-2 \sqrt{2} \cos x+\cos ^{2} x+3 \cos ^{2} x-3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 \cos ^{2} x-2 \sqrt{2} \cos x-1=0 \Rightarrow \cos x=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}+\sqrt{8+16}}{8}=\frac{2 \sqrt{2} \pm 2 \sqrt{6}}{8}=\frac{\sqrt{2} \pm \sqrt{6}}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=15^{\circ} \text { or } 105^{\circ}=(24 n+1) \frac{\pi}{5} \text { or }(24 n+7) \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #44 (ID: 442994)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2008-2009, 2005-2006 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x[/math]এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \quad \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
ধরি[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta=x \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
আবার ধরি, [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \Rightarrow \theta^{\prime}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই,[math]\sin \sin ^{-1} x=x[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #45 (ID: 442995)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2012-2013, 2008-2009, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (B Unit), Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math] হয় [math]\tan \theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]9[/math]
B. [math]7 / 2[/math]
C. [math]3 / 5[/math]
D. [math]4 / 5[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { }\left(\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3\right)+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 4+\tan ^{-1}(-1)=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{4+(-1)}{1-4(-1)}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #46 (ID: 442984)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University of Engineering & Technology

Question:
[math]\operatorname{costan}^{-1} \operatorname{cotsin}^{-1} x[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
E. [math]\frac{1}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos \tan ^{-1} \cot \sin ^{-1} x=\cos \tan ^{-1} \cot \left[\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)\right][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\cos \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)=\cos \cos ^{-1}(x)=x \\\text { Alternate: } \cos \tan _{1} \cot \sin ^{-1} x=\cos \tan ^{-1} \tan \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} x\right) \\\Rightarrow\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} x\right)=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} x\right)=x\end{array}[/math]

Question #48 (ID: 442986)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{x} \sin \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] এবং [math]\sqrt{x} \cos \theta=1[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত হবে ?

Options:
A. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]90^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{x} \sin \theta=\sqrt{3} ; \sin \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{x}} \therefore \frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan 60^{\circ} \therefore \theta=60^{\circ}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #49 (ID: 442987)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]\theta[/math] একটি ধনাত্মক সূক্ষ্মকোণ যেখানে সমীকরণ [math]2 \sin ^{2} \theta=3 \cos \theta ; \theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]90^{\circ}[/math]
খ. [math]120^{\circ}[/math]
গ. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
ঘ. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\text { Using Calculator. } 2 \times\left[\sin (60)^{2}\right\}=3 / 2 ; 3 \times \cos 60^{\circ}=3 / 2[/math]

Question:
বিপরীত বৃত্তীয় ফাংশন, [math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]12[/math]
B. [math]-15[/math]
C. [math]3[/math]
D. [math]15[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=\left(\sec \sec ^{-1}(\sqrt{5})\right)^{2} +\left(\operatorname{cosec} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}(\sqrt{10})\right)^{2}=5+10=15[/math]

Question #53 (ID: 442991)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Chattogram Veterinary and Animal Sciences University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f(x)=\sin x \cos ^{3} x[/math] হলে [math]f(\pi+x)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\cos x \cos ^{3} x[/math]
B. [math]-\sin x \cos ^{3} x[/math]
C. [math]-\cos x \cos ^{3} x[/math]
D. [math]\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]f(x)=\sin x \cos ^{3} x ; f(x+x)=\sin (\pi+x) \cos ^{3}(\pi+x)=-\sin x\left(-\cos ^{3} x\right)=\sin x \cos ^{3} x=f(x)[/math]

Explanation:

Question #54 (ID: 442992)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021-2022, 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]n \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \times \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #55 (ID: 442993)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #56 (ID: 442979)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \tan ^{-1} \sin \cot ^{-1} x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}}[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{1-x^{2}}}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1-x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\theta_{1}=\cos ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} \therefore \text { sincot }^{-1} x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\theta_{2}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}} \therefore \cos ^{-1} x=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \operatorname{costan}^{-1} \operatorname{sincot}^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\sqrt{\frac{1+x^{2}}{2+x^{2}}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #67 (ID: 442951)

C. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]0^{\circ}[/math]
E. No correct answer

 হলে [math]\cos \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 হতে পারে না; কারণ- [math]\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta=\frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{5}=\frac{4}{5} 
eq 1[/math]

Question #68 (ID: 442952)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin A+\cos A=\sin B+\cos B[/math] হলে নিচের কোনটি সঠিক

Options:
ক. [math]A-B=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]A+B=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]A+ B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]A-B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin A+\cos A=\sin B+\cos B \Rightarrow \sin A-\sin B=\cos B-\cos A[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \cos \frac{A+B}{2} \sin \frac{A-B}{2}=2 \sin \frac{A+B}{2} \sin \frac{A-B}{2} \Rightarrow \tan \frac{A+B}{2}=1 \Rightarrow \frac{A+B}{2}=\frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #70 (ID: 442954)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { If } 2 \cos ^{2} \theta-\sin \theta=1 \text { then the value of } \theta \text { is- }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6} \text { and } \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\text { Both a and b }[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]2 \cos ^{2} \theta-\sin \theta=1 \Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\sin \theta-1=0 \Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2},-1=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}, \sin \left(-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{\pi}{2}\right) \text { putting } n=0,1 \text { we get, } \theta=\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

[math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-x}{1+x}=?[/math]

B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\text { Both } a \text { and } c[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1-x}{1+x}=\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} 1-\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Question #73 (ID: 442957)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot x-\tan x=2[/math] - সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান-

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{8}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cot x-\tan x=2 \Rightarrow \frac{\cos x}{\sin x}-\frac{\sin x}{\cos x}=2 \Rightarrow \frac{\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x}{2 \sin x \cos x}=1 \Rightarrow \frac{\cos 2 x}{\sin 2 x}=1 \Rightarrow \tan 2 x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1 \Rightarrow \tan \frac{\pi}{4}=\tan 2 x[/math]
 এবং [math]2 x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow x=\frac{\pi}{8} \Rightarrow x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #74 (ID: 442958)

Question #75 (ID: 442959)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \cos ^{-1} x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(1-2 x^{2}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(3 x-4 x^{3}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}-1\right)[/math]
D. [math]\cos ^{-1}\left(4 x^{3}-3 x\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]2 \cos ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1}\left(x \cdot x-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \cdot \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left\{x^{2}-\left(1-x^{2}\right)\right\}=\cos ^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}-1\right)[/math]
 [ বি:দ্র [math]2 \cos ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}-1\right) ; 2 \sin ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1}\left(1-2 x^{2}\right)[/math]
 সূত্র ]

Explanation:

Question #76 (ID: 442960)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান হবে -

Options:
A. [math]\frac{5}{6}[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{5}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1- \frac{1}{3}\frac{1}{2}}\right)=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{6-1}\right) \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}=1[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর সাধারন মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi \pm(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{3} ; \tan \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} ; \tan \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{6}=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x=\sqrt{-1}[/math] হলে [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(x^{4}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]0[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x=\sqrt{-1} \Rightarrow x^{2}=-1, \Rightarrow x^{4}=(-1)^{2}=1 \therefore \tan ^{-1}\left(x^{4}\right)=\tan ^{-1}(1)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Question #80 (ID: 442964)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jhenidah Government Veterinary College, Chattogram Veterinary and Animal Sciences University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]ABC[/math] ত্রিভুজে [math]\sin ^{-1} \tan (A+B+C)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]0^{\circ}[/math]
খ. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]
গ. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
ঘ. [math]90^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\triangle \mathrm{ABC}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} \tan (A+B+C)=\sin ^{-1} \tan \pi=\sin ^{-1} 0=\sin ^{-1} \sin 0^{\circ}=0^{\circ}[/math]

Question #81 (ID: 442965)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jhenidah Government Veterinary College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{9}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2}{9}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)-1+\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \Rightarrow 1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}-1+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{9}=\frac{2}{9}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #83 (ID: 442967)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]2[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{3}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{5} / 2[/math]
E. [math]\sqrt{3} / 2[/math]


Correct Answer: E.[math]\sqrt{3} / 2[/math]

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{Hints: } \sin 30^{\circ}=\frac{1}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=30^{\circ}\\\text{Solve: } \sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=\sin \left(2.30^{\circ}\right)=\sin 60^{\circ}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:
আরেক ভাবে অংকটা করা যায়: আমরা জানি, [math]\sin 2 \theta=2 \sin \theta \cos \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=2 \sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \cos \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\=2 \frac{1}{2} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Question #84 (ID: 442968)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2018-2019, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Sylhet Agricultural University, University of Barishal (UNIT A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #85 (ID: 442969)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2006-2007, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 x \tan x=1[/math] হলে x এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 x \tan x=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan ^{2} x}{1-\tan ^{2} x}=1 \Rightarrow \tan x=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \Rightarrow x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #90 (ID: 442974)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{x}^{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]2 x[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta \text { Or, } x=\tan \theta[/math]
 [math]\sin 2 \theta=\frac{2+\tan \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta} \quad \text { Or, } \sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \text { Or, } 2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \quad \text { Or, } 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #91 (ID: 442934)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \tan ^{-1} \cot \sin ^{-1} x[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math]
D. [math]x[/math]
E. [math]\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{costan}^{-1} \cot \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\text { Let, } \sin ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \sin \theta=x \therefore \cot \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \therefore \theta \cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)[/math]
তাহলে [math]\cos \tan ^{-1} \cot \left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)=\cos \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)[/math]
 [math]\text { Let, } \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime} \Rightarrow \tan \theta^{\prime}=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \quad \therefore \theta^{\prime}=\cos ^{-1}(x) \therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই [math]\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} x\right)=x[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #94 (ID: 442937)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta=x[/math] হলে [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=x \Rightarrow \theta=\sin ^{-1} x \quad \text { Now, } \operatorname{Sin}^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\theta+\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=?[/math]

A. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]

Question #99 (ID: 442942)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin x=\cos x[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin x=\cos x \Rightarrow \sin x-\cos x=0 \Rightarrow \sin (x)-\sin \left(\frac{x}{2}-x\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \times \sin \left(\frac{x-\frac{\pi}{2}+x}{2}\right) \times \cos \left(\frac{x+\frac{\pi}{2}-x}{2}\right)=0 \Rightarrow \sin \left(x-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=0 \Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{4}=0,180^{\circ}..........[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{5 \pi}{4}, \ldots[/math]


Explanation:

Question #100 (ID: 442943)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin A+\cos A=\sin B+\cos B[/math] এবং [math]A=\frac{2 \pi}{9}[/math] হলে [math]B=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{9}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{9}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{7 \pi}{18}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{18}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin A+\cos A=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-A\right)+\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-A\right)=\cos B+\cos B ; \text { Comparing, } B=\frac{\pi}{2}-A ; B=\frac{5 \pi}{18}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #102 (ID: 442945)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta-\cos 2 \theta=2[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{n}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta-\left(1-2 \sin ^{2} \theta\right)=2 \Rightarrow \sin \theta-1+2 \sin ^{2} \theta=2 \Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta+\sin \theta-3=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=1,-\frac{3}{2}[/math]
 কিন্তু [math]\sin \theta=-\frac{3}{2}[/math]
 গ্রহণযোগ্য নয় [math]\therefore \sin \theta=1 \therefore \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #103 (ID: 442946)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { In the case of triangle } A B C \text { if } a^{4}+b^{4}+c^{4}=2 c^{2}\left(a^{2}+b^{2}\right) \text {, the value of } \cos C \text { will be- }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\pm \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of them }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]a^{4}+b^{4}+c^{4}=2 c^{2}\left(a^{2}+b^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(a^{2}\right)^{2}+\left(b^{2}\right)^{2}+\left(-c^{2}\right)^{2}+2 a^{2}\left(-c^{2}\right)+2 b^{2}\left(-c^{2}\right)+2 a^{2} b^{2}=2 a^{2} b^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(a^{2}+b^{2}-c^{2}\right)^{2}=2 a^{2} b^{2} \Rightarrow a^{2}+b^{2}-c^{2}=\pm \sqrt{2} a b \Rightarrow \frac{a^{2}+b^{2}-c^{2}}{2 a b}=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \therefore\cos C=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #104 (ID: 442947)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2018-2019, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Sylhet Agricultural University, University of Barishal (UNIT A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #105 (ID: 442948)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Chittagong (Unit A), University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #106 (ID: 442927)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2008-2009, 2005-2006 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x[/math]এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \quad \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
ধরি[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta=x \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
আবার ধরি, [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \Rightarrow \theta^{\prime}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই,[math]\sin \sin ^{-1} x=x[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #107 (ID: 442928)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2(\sec x+\cos x)=5[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]2(\sec x+\cos x)=5 \Rightarrow \frac{2\left(1+\cos ^{2} x\right)}{\cos x}=5 \Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x-5 \cos x+2=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(2 \cos x-1)(\cos x-2)=0 \therefore \cos x=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{3}\right)[/math]
যেহেতু [math]\cos x 
eq 2 \therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

ঘ. [math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math] এর ডোমেন [math]R-\{n \pi, n \in Z\}[/math]

Question #110 (ID: 442931)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2(\sin \theta \cos \theta+\sqrt{3})=\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+4 \sin 0 ; 0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] এর সমাধান বের কর।

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]2(\sin \theta \cos \theta+\sqrt{3})=\sqrt{3} \cos \theta+4 \sin \theta \Rightarrow 2 \sin \theta \cos \theta-4 \sin \theta+2 \sqrt{3}-\sqrt{3} \cos \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \sin \theta(\cos \theta-2)-\sqrt{3}(\cos \theta-2)=0 \Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[\cos \theta 
eq 2] \Rightarrow \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #111 (ID: 442932)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Chattogram Veterinary and Animal Sciences University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} P=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{2}\right)[/math] হলে [math]P[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}-x[/math]
B. [math]\frac{x}{2}[/math]
C. [math]x[/math]
D. [math]2x[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} P=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{2} \therefore P=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{2}\right)=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{2}=\frac{x}{2}[/math]

Question #112 (ID: 442933)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2012-2013, 2008-2009, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (B Unit), Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math] হয় [math]\tan \theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]9[/math]
B. [math]7 / 2[/math]
C. [math]3 / 5[/math]
D. [math]4 / 5[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { }\left(\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3\right)+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 4+\tan ^{-1}(-1)=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{4+(-1)}{1-4(-1)}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #113 (ID: 442921)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x[/math]এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \quad \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
ধরি[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta=x \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
আবার ধরি, [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \Rightarrow \theta^{\prime}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই,[math]\sin \sin ^{-1} x=x[/math]

[Image]

Question #114 (ID: 442922)

Question:
[math]\sin \theta-\cos \theta=0[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর ক্ষুদ্রতম ধনাত্মক মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta-\cos \theta=0 \Rightarrow \sin \theta=\cos \theta \Rightarrow \tan \theta=1 \quad \therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ক্ষুদ্রতম ধনাত্মক মানের জন্য [math]\mathrm{n}=0 \quad \therefore \theta[/math]
 এর নির্ণেয় মান [math]=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}[/math] সমান-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\=\tan ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]

Question #118 (ID: 442926)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019, 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে [math]\theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1 \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \Rightarrow \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} x\right)=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]

Question #125 (ID: 442899)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021, 2014-2015, 2021-2022, 2017-2018, 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Agriculture Cluster Admission System, Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=-1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #126 (ID: 442900)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2018-2019, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Sylhet Agricultural University, University of Barishal (UNIT A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]

Question #128 (ID: 442902)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021-2022, 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]n \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \times \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\tan 2 x \tan x=1[/math] হলে x এর মান কত ?

Options:

খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 x \tan x=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan ^{2} x}{1-\tan ^{2} x}=1 \Rightarrow \tan x=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \Rightarrow x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]

Question #130 (ID: 442904)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #133 (ID: 442907)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\cos (\pi \sin \theta)[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] -এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1} \cdot\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{2}+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]
D. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{2}+\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]
E. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-3}\left(\frac{1}{1 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\cos (\pi \sin \theta) \Rightarrow \sin (\pi \cos \theta)=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta\right) \Rightarrow \pi \cos \theta=\frac{\pi}{2} \pm \pi \sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta \pm \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}} \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}} \Rightarrow \theta=\pm \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #136 (ID: 442910)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology, Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{a}}{1+\mathrm{a}^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{b}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{b}^{2}}[/math] সমীকরণে x এর মান হল-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 a b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} x=2 \tan ^{-1} a+2 \tan ^{-1} b=2\left(\tan ^{-1} a+\tan ^{-1} b\right) \text \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{a+b}{1-a b} \quad \therefore x=\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]

Question #141 (ID: 442915)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
n পূর্ণ সংখ্যা হলে, [math]\sin \left\{n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}\right\}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]
E. [math]-\frac{1}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin \left(\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\sin \frac{5 \pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin \left(-\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
এভাবে n এর যত মান বসানো যায় ফলাফল একই আসে।

Explanation:
অঙ্কটায় n এর মান পছন্দমতো যেকোন পূর্ণসংখ্যা বসিয়ে Check করলে মান একই আসবে। প্রত্যেকক্ষেত্রে যে মান আসবে সেটিই সঠিক উত্তর।By Calculator: n এর মান বসিয়ে Check করার কাজটুকু চাইলে ক্যালকুলেটরের মাধ্যমে করতে পারো। n = 1 মনে করে ফাংশনটি ক্যালকুলেটরে [math]\sin \left\{1 \pi+(-1)^{1} \frac{\pi}{6}\right\}[/math]
 আকারে Input দিয়ে সমান চাপলে মান আসে - এরপর n এর অন্য মান বসিয়েও চেক করে দেখতে পারো একই মান আসবে। মনে রাখবে, ক্যালকুলেটরে [math]\pi[/math]
 কে সরাসরি [math]\pi[/math]
 আকারেই Input দিতে চাইলে ক্যালকুলেটরকে Radian মুডে রাখতে হবে। Degree রাখলে [math]\pi[/math]
 কে 180 হিসেবে Input দিতে হবে।

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \theta+\cot \theta=2[/math] হলে [math]\theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\theta=(2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan \theta+\frac{1}{\tan \theta}=2 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta-2 \tan \theta+1=0 \Rightarrow \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #150 (ID: 442888)

Question:
যদি [math]\tan ^{-1} a+\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}=\pi[/math] হয়, তাহলে [math]a + b + c[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]5 \mathrm{abc}[/math]
B. [math]7 \mathrm{abc}[/math]
C. [math]11 \mathrm{abc}[/math]
D. [math]2 \mathrm{abc}[/math]
E. [math]\text { abc }[/math]


Correct Answer: E.[math]\text { abc }[/math]

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} a+\frac{1}{2} \sec ^{-1} \frac{1+b^{2}}{1-b^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1+c^{2}}{2 c}=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}+\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2} \cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{b}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{b}^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 c}{1+\mathrm{c}^{2}}=\pi \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{~b}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{c}=\pi \\\Rightarrow \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{~b}+\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{c}\right)=\tan \left(\pi-\tan ^{-1} \mathrm{a}\right) \Rightarrow \frac{\mathrm{b}+\mathrm{c}}{1-\mathrm{bc}}=-\mathrm{a} \Rightarrow \mathrm{b}+\mathrm{c}=\mathrm{abc}-\mathrm{a} \\\therefore \mathrm{abc}=\mathrm{a}+\mathrm{b}+\mathrm{c}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #154 (ID: 442892)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]y=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left[\frac{\sqrt{1+\sin x}-\sqrt{1-\sin x}}{\sqrt{1+\sin x}+\sqrt{1-\sin x}}\right][/math] এর সঠিক সংক্ষিপ্ত রূপ কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]x=2 y[/math]
B. [math]x=4 y[/math]
C. [math]x+4 y=0[/math]
D. [math]x-y=0[/math]
E. [math]x+2 y=0[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]y=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left[\frac{\sqrt{1+\sin x}-\sqrt{1-\sin x}}{\sqrt{1+\sin x}+\sqrt{1-\sin x}}\right]=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left[\frac{2 \sin \frac{x}{2}}{2 \cos \frac{x}{2}}\right]=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left[\tan \frac{x}{2}\right]=\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{x}{2}=\frac{x}{4} \therefore x=4 y[/math]

Question #156 (ID: 442875)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021, 2014-2015, 2021-2022, 2017-2018, 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Agriculture Cluster Admission System, Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=-1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #157 (ID: 442876)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
B. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
C. [math]x+y=1[/math]
D. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\sqrt{1-\mathrm{y}^{2}} \Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}=1-\mathrm{y}^{2} \Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #158 (ID: 442877)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: Patuakhali Science And Technology University (A Unit)

Question:
যদি [math]\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] তবে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]2[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} y \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\cos ^{-1} y\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y \Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]

Question #162 (ID: 442881)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{y}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{y+\sqrt{\left(y^{2}-x^{2}\right)}}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{x+\sqrt{y^{2}-x^{2}}}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\left(\frac{y-x}{y+x}\right)}\right)[/math]
D. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\left(\frac{y+x}{y-x}\right)}\right)[/math]
E. [math]2 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{x+\sqrt{\left(x^{2}-y^{2}\right)}}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
Hints: [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{x}{y}[/math]
 কে [math]\theta[/math]
 ধরে নিয়ে tan [math]\theta[/math]
 থেকে calculation শুরু করো।Solve: [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{y}\right)=\theta \quad \therefore \frac{x}{y}=\sin 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\tan \theta=\frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 \sin \theta \cos \theta}{2 \cos ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [লব ও হরকে 2cose দ্বারা গুণ][math]=\frac{\sin 2 \theta}{1+\cos 2 \theta}=\frac{\sin 2 \theta}{1+\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} 2 \theta}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\frac{x}{y}}{1+\sqrt{1-\frac{x^{2}}{y^{2}}}}=\frac{\frac{x}{y}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{y^{2}-x^{2}}{y^{2}}}}=\frac{x}{y+\sqrt{y^{2}-x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{x}{y+\sqrt{y^{2}-x^{2}}}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

 [এক্ষেত্রে কোণগুলো ধনাত্মক বলে এদের যোগফল 0 বা [math]\pi[/math]

 [math]\\[/math]

Question #170 (ID: 442852)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]
