Question #172 (ID: 442854)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{5}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

B. [math]\frac{1}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

 [math]=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{6-1}\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]

Question #176 (ID: 442858)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin A=\frac{1}{2}[/math] এবং [math]\cos B=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\tan \mathrm{A} \tan \mathrm{B}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan A \tan B=\frac{\sin A \sin B}{\cos A \cos B}=\frac{\frac{1}{2} \sqrt{1-\frac{1}{3}}}{\sqrt{1 \frac{1}{4}} \times \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}}=\frac{\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}}{\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \times \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #177 (ID: 442859)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]7 \sin ^{2} \theta+3 \cos ^{2} \theta=4[/math] হলে [math]\sec \theta[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]7\left(1-\cos ^{2} \theta\right)+3 \cos ^{2} \theta=4 \Rightarrow 7-7 \cos ^{2} \theta+3 \cos ^{2} \theta-4=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 3-4 \cos ^{2} \theta=0 \Rightarrow \cos ^{2} \theta=\frac{3}{4} \Rightarrow \sec ^{2} \theta=\frac{4}{3} \therefore \sec \theta=\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #178 (ID: 442860)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \cos ^{2} \theta+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta=3[/math] হলে [math]\theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]20^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]10^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]2 \cos ^{2} \theta+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-3=0 \Rightarrow 2\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-3=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow-2 \sin ^{2} \theta+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1=0 \Rightarrow(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta)^{2}-2 \times \sqrt{2} \sin \theta \times 1+(1)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1)^{2}=0 \therefore \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \therefore \theta=45^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #186 (ID: 442868)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]5 / 6[/math]
D. [math]-5 / 6[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1 \frac{1}{3} \times \frac{1}{2}}=\frac{\frac{5}{6}}{\frac{5}{6}}=1[/math]

Question #187 (ID: 442869)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 1+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\pi[/math]
D. [math]2 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1}(1)+\tan ^{-1}(2)+\tan ^{-1}(3)=\tan ^{-1}(1)+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{3 \pi}{4}=\pi[/math]

Explanation:

Question #188 (ID: 442870)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot ^{2} \theta-(\sqrt{3}+1) \cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0,0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}, \theta=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\cot ^{2} \theta-\sqrt{3} \cot \theta-\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0 \Rightarrow \cot \theta(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})-1(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})(\cot \theta-1)=0 \Rightarrow \theta=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #189 (ID: 442871)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]0 \leq \theta \leq \frac{\pi}{2}[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \sin \theta+\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \cos \theta=1 \Rightarrow \sin \left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 হবে [math]\left[\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=1\right][/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #190 (ID: 442872)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \theta+\tan 2 \theta+\sqrt{3} \tan \theta \tan 2 \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত? (যখন [math]n[/math] এর মান শূন্য বা পূর্ণসংখ্যা)

Options:
A. [math]\frac{n}{3}+\frac{\pi}{7}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{7}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{n}{3}+\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan \theta+\tan 2 \theta=\sqrt{3}(1-\tan \theta \tan 2 \theta) \Rightarrow \frac{\tan \theta+\tan 2 \theta}{1-\tan \theta \tan 2 \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan 3 \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow 3 \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} \quad \therefore \theta=\frac{n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #192 (ID: 442833)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2022-2023, 2021-2022 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{11}[/math]
B. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}\end{array}[/math]
[Image]


Explanation:

Question #193 (ID: 442834)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}[/math] সমান-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\arctan \left\{\sin \left(\arccos \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\} \\=\tan ^{-1}\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #194 (ID: 442835)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2018-2019, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Sylhet Agricultural University, University of Barishal (UNIT A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #195 (ID: 442836)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021-2022, 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]n \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \times \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #196 (ID: 442837)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #197 (ID: 442838)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \frac{1}{3}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{5}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #198 (ID: 442839)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot \theta-\tan \theta=\frac{\mathbf{6}}{\mathbf{5}}[/math] হলে, 1 নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{\sqrt{34}}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{34}}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{10}{\sqrt{34}}\right)[/math]
D. [math]\theta=\frac{1}{5} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{34}}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot \theta-\tan \theta=\frac{6}{5}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}-\frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}=\frac{6}{5} \Rightarrow \frac{\cos ^{2} \theta-\sin ^{2} \theta}{2 \sin \theta \cos \theta}=\frac{3}{5} \Rightarrow \frac{\cos 2 \theta}{\sin 2 \theta}=\frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \cot 2 \theta=\frac{3}{5} \Rightarrow 2 \theta=\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{5}\right) \Rightarrow \theta=\frac{1}{2} \cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{5}\right) \\\therefore \theta=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{34}}\right)\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]14[/math]
B. [math]15[/math]
C. [math]16[/math]
D. [math]17[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)[/math]
 [math]=1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=2+2^{2}+3^{2}=2+4+9=15[/math]

Question #200 (ID: 442841)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023, 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} x=2[/math] এবং [math]\cot x=-\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\cos x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\tan x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\sec x=-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cot x=-\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \tan x=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{3}[/math]

Question #201 (ID: 442842)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]0 \leq x \leq \frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\sqrt{3} \cos x+\sin x=1[/math] 1 এর সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]x=0[/math]
B. [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]x=\frac{-3 \pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos x+\frac{1}{2} \sin x=\frac{1}{2} \Rightarrow \cos x \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\sin x \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \left(x-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow x-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #202 (ID: 442843)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Agriculture Cluster Admission System
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি , [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math] হয় তবে [math]\tan \theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]9[/math]
B. [math]\frac{7}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3+4-2 \times 3 \times 4}{1-2 \times 3-3 \times 4-4 \times 2}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta \therefore \tan \theta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #203 (ID: 442844)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan x+\tan 3 x=0[/math] -এর সমাধান কী হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan x+\tan 3 x=\tan 4 x(1-\tan x \tan 3 x)=0 \therefore \tan 4 x=0=\tan (n \pi) \therefore x=\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]

Question #206 (ID: 442847)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} y[/math] সমীকরণে [math]x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math] হলে y এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]2 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} y \Rightarrow 2 \times \frac{\pi}{3}=\sin ^{-1} y\left[\because \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=x \therefore \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{3}\right][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow y=\sin \frac{2 \pi}{3} \therefore y=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #207 (ID: 442816)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021 | Boards:  | Tags: General and Science Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি, [math]\sec ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} x[/math] হ্য,তবে X এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]3[/math]
খ. [math]\sqrt{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\tan ^{-1} x=\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{1+x^{2}}=\sec ^{-1} 3 \Rightarrow \sqrt{1+x^{2}}=3 \Rightarrow 1+x^{2}=9 \Rightarrow x^{2}=8 \Rightarrow x=2 \sqrt{2}[/math]
বিকল্প সমাধান :
[Image]
[math]\sqrt{3^{2}-1^{2}}=2 \sqrt{2} \therefore \sec ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} x \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{1}\right) \Rightarrow x=2 \sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #209 (ID: 442818)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. $ [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] $
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
C. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{13}{16}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{13}{16}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times \frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\frac{1}{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \times \frac{1}{7}}\\=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} 1\\=\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{8}[/math]


Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (C Unit)

B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]

Question #212 (ID: 442821)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{3} \cos A+\sin A=2(0

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{3} \cos A+\sin A=2 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \cos A+\frac{1}{2} \sin A=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos A \cos \frac{\pi}{6}+\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \sin A=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \left(A-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\cos 0^{\circ} \\\Rightarrow A-\frac{\pi}{6}=0 \\\Rightarrow A=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #213 (ID: 442822)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{3} \sin y-\cos y=2(0

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\pi[/math]
C. [math]\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{3} \sin y-\cos y=2 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin y-\frac{1}{2} \cos y=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\pi}{3} \sin y-\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \cos y=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos y \cos \frac{\pi}{3}-\sin y \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=-1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(y+\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=\cos \pi \\\Rightarrow y+\frac{\pi}{3}=\pi \\\Rightarrow y=\pi-\frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #214 (ID: 442823)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]5 \tan ^{2} \theta-\sec ^ {2} \theta=11(\pi<\theta<2 \pi)[/math] সমীকরণ [math]\theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{7 \pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]5 \tan ^{2} \theta-\sec ^{2} \theta=11 \\\Rightarrow 5 \tan ^{2} \theta-\left(1+\tan ^{2} \theta\right)=11[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 \tan ^{2} \theta=12 \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=3 \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\sqrt{3}=\tan \frac{\pi}{3}=\tan\left(\pi+\frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow \theta=\pi+\frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{4 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #216 (ID: 442825)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{1+b^{2}}[/math] সমীকরণে x এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 a b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
D. কোনোটিই নয়


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{x}}{1-\mathrm{x}^{2}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \mathrm{a}}{1+\mathrm{a}^{2}}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-\mathrm{b}^{2}}{1+\mathrm{b}^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{-1} x=2 \tan ^{-1} a+2 \tan ^{-1} b \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} a+\tan ^{-1} b \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{a+b}{1-a b}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]

Question:
[math]\tan{2\theta} \tan{\theta} = 1[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান হলো:

Options:
ক. [math]2n\pi + \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2n\pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]n\pi + \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n\pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

 → সমাধান: [math]2\theta = 90^\circ - \theta \Rightarrow 3\theta = 90^\circ \Rightarrow \theta = 30^\circ = \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 → তাই সাধারণ সমাধান [math]n\pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]

 [math]=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{6-1}\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=-1\end{array}[/math]

Question #223 (ID: 442832)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #225 (ID: 442793)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { If } \sin A+\cos A=\sin B+\cos B \text { then } A+B=\text { ? }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\pi[/math]
B. [math]\pi / 2[/math]
C. [math]2 \pi[/math]
D. [math]\pi / 4[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin A+\cos A=\sin B+\cos B \Rightarrow \sin A-\sin B=\cos B-\cos A[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{A-B}{2} \cos \frac{A+B}{2}=2 \sin \frac{A+B}{2} \sin \frac{A-B}{2} \Rightarrow \tan \frac{A+B}{2}=1 \therefore A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #226 (ID: 442794)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান-

Options:
A. [math]\theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta \Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}+\sqrt{3}=\frac{2}{\sin \theta} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\pi}{6} \cdot \cos \theta+\cos \frac{\pi}{6} \cdot \sin \theta=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{6}+\theta\right)=1 \Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{6}+\theta=\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{2} ;[n \in \mathbb{Z}] \\\Rightarrow \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{6}=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2018-2019, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Sylhet Agricultural University, University of Barishal (UNIT A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5/6[/math]
B. [math]6/5[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3π/4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right),\left[\frac{1}{6}<1\right][/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \times \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\frac{5}{6-1}=\frac{5}{5}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question:

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \tan ^{-1} 1+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3 \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1+2+3-1 \times 2 \times 3}{1-1 \times 2-2 \times 3-3 \times 1}\right)=\pi+\tan ^{-1}(0)=\pi+0=\pi\end{array}[/math]

Question #230 (ID: 442798)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot x-\tan x=2[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান-

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{n \pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{8}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{\tan x}-\tan x=2 \Rightarrow 1-\tan ^{2} x-2 \tan x \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x+2 \tan x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan x=-1+\sqrt{2} ;-1-\sqrt{2} ; \tan x=-1+\sqrt{2}[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{(4 n+1) \pi}{8}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #231 (ID: 442799)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021-2022, 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]n \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \times \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]

Question #234 (ID: 442802)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y[/math] হ্য,তবে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}[/math] এর মান হবে -

Options:
ক. [math]2[/math]
খ. [math]1[/math]
গ. [math]-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y \Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} y=\sin ^{-1} x \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]

Question #235 (ID: 442803)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
সমাধান কর : [math]2 \tan ^{-1}(\cos x)=\tan ^{-1}(2 \operatorname{cosec} x)[/math]

Options:
A. [math]n \pi \pm(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1}(\cos x)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cos x}{1-\cos ^{2} x} \therefore \frac{2 \cos x}{\sin ^{2} x}=\frac{2}{\sin x} \Rightarrow \tan x=1 \Rightarrow x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Question #236 (ID: 442804)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2,\left(0^{\circ}<\theta<360^{\circ}\right)[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।

Options:
A. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]90^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]120^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2 \Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \Rightarrow \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} \\\mathrm{n}=0, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{z} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow 60^{\circ}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #237 (ID: 442805)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি, [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয় তাহলে [math]\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)[/math] এর মান হচ্ছে -

Options:
A. [math]4[/math]
B. [math]3[/math]
C. [math]2[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #239 (ID: 442807)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024, 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)

Question:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \pi[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times \frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{7}}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{25}{25}=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Question #243 (ID: 442811)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Chittagong (Unit A), University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

যদি [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হয়, তবে [math]x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}+y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math] এর মান নির্ণয় কর।

A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{4}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]
E. [math]\text { None of them }[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}+y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}+y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}=\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=1[/math]

Question #247 (ID: 442815)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: General and Science Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\tan \left(\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)=\sin \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math] হয় তাহলে x = ?

Options:
A. [math]\pm \frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)=\sin \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \Rightarrow \frac{1-x^{2}}{x^{2}}=\frac{4}{5} \\\Rightarrow 5-5 x^{2}=4 x^{2} \Rightarrow 9 x^{2}=5 \Rightarrow x^{2}=\frac{5}{9} \\\therefore x= \pm \frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #250 (ID: 442780)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018-2019 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{13}}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\tan ^{-1}(5 / 9)[/math]
B. [math]\tan ^{-1}(3 / 7)[/math]
C. [math]\pi / 2[/math]
D. [math]\pi / 4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{2}{3}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\cot \theta \cot 3 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান-

Options:
ক. [math](2 n+1) \pi / 4[/math]
খ. [math](2 n+1) \pi / 8[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi / 4[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n-1) \pi / 2[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{\cos \theta \cos 3 \theta}{\sin \theta \sin 3 \theta}=1 \Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos 3 \theta-\sin \theta \sin 3 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos (\theta+3 \theta)=0 \Rightarrow \cos 4 \theta=0 \Rightarrow 4 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #252 (ID: 442782)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
B. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
C. [math]x+y=1[/math]
D. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\sqrt{1-\mathrm{y}^{2}} \Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}=1-\mathrm{y}^{2} \Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #253 (ID: 442783)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]0 \leq x \leq 90^{\circ}[/math] হলে [math]\sin 3 x=\cos x[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]0^{\circ}, 45^{\circ}[/math]
খ. [math]0^{\circ}, 22.5^{\circ}[/math]
গ. [math]45^{\circ}, 45^{\circ}[/math]
ঘ. [math]22.5^{\circ}, 45^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin 3 x=\cos x \Rightarrow \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 x\right)=\cos x \Rightarrow x=2 n \pi \pm\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 x\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=2 n \pi+\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-3 x\right) ; x=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}-3 x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 \mathrm{x}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{2} ; \mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=22.5^{\circ}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } x=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}+3 x \Rightarrow 2 x=\frac{\pi}{2}-2 n \pi[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=45^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #254 (ID: 442784)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021, 2014-2015, 2021-2022, 2017-2018, 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Agriculture Cluster Admission System, Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=-1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #255 (ID: 442785)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #256 (ID: 442786)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)

Question:
[math]\text { If } \tan ^{-1}\left(x+\frac{1}{3}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(x-\frac{1}{3}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 2 \text {, then the value of } x \text { is }-[/math]

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{5}{6}[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{1}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1}\left(x+\frac{1}{3}\right)+\tan ^{-1}\left(x-\frac{1}{3}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+\frac{1}{3}+x-\frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(x+\frac{1}{3}\right)\left(x-\frac{1}{3}\right)}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{\frac{10}{9}-x^{2}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 x=\frac{20}{9}-2 x^{2} \Rightarrow 9 x^{2}+9 x-10=0 \therefore x=\frac{2}{3},-\frac{5}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #258 (ID: 442788)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{85}{36}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{36}{85}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{10}{9}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{9}{10}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)=\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right)+1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}+1+\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}=1+\frac{1}{9}+1+\frac{1}{4}=\frac{72+4+9}{36}=\frac{85}{36}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #259 (ID: 442789)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)

Question:
[math]\text { If } \cot ^{2} \theta-(\sqrt{3}+1) \cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0,0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2} \text {, then } \theta=\text { ? }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}, \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{\pi}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\cot ^{2} \theta-(\sqrt{3}+1) \cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0 \Rightarrow \cot ^{2} \theta-\sqrt{3} \cot \theta-\cot \theta+\sqrt{3}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cot \theta(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})-1(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})=0 \Rightarrow(\cot \theta-\sqrt{3})(\cot \theta-1)=0 \\\therefore \cot \theta=\sqrt{3}, \cot \theta=1 \Rightarrow \theta=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #261 (ID: 442773)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024, 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \pi[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \times \frac{1}{3}}{1-\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{1}{7}}{1-\frac{3}{4} \cdot \frac{1}{7}}\\=\tan ^{-1} \frac{25}{25}=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #263 (ID: 442775)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (C  Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]5 \tan ^{2} x-\sec ^{2} x=11[/math] হলে, [math]x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+(1) \frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]5 \tan ^{2} x-\sec ^{2} x=11 \Rightarrow 5 \tan ^{2} x-\left(1+\tan ^{2} x\right)=11[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 5 \tan ^{2} x-1-\tan ^{2} x-11=0 \Rightarrow 4 \tan ^{2} x=12 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x=3 \\\Rightarrow \tan x= \pm \sqrt{3}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \Rightarrow x=n \pi+\left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #264 (ID: 442776)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \theta+\cot \theta=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta, 0 \leq \theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan \theta+\cot \theta=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta \Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\frac{2}{\sin \theta}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta}{\cos \theta \sin \theta}=\frac{2}{\sin \theta} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos \theta}=2 \Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #270 (ID: 442767)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের ভিত্তিতে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]\cot \theta=\mathbf{k}[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\alpha[/math] [math]k=1[/math] এবং [math]\frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot \theta=\mathrm{k} \Rightarrow \cot \theta=1 \quad[\because \mathrm{k}=1] \\\Rightarrow\cot \theta=\cot \frac{\pi}{4} \therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=0+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
যেহেতু [math]\frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
 সুতরাং [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #271 (ID: 442768)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: DU 7 College Admission Exam
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}+2[/math]


Explanation:

Question #278 (ID: 442743)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos x+\sec x=2[/math] হলে x এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
খ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos x+\sec x=2 \\\Rightarrow \cos x+\frac{1}{\cos x}=2 \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{2} x-2 \cos x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos x=1 \therefore x=2 n \pi\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #279 (ID: 442744)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin x \cos x=\frac{1}{4}[/math] হলে x এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin x \cdot \cos x=\frac{1}{4} \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x \cdot \cos x=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\Rightarrow 2 x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore x=\frac{n \pi}{2}+(-1)^{n} \cdot \frac{\pi}{12}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #282 (ID: 442747)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018 | Boards:  | Tags: All Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\triangle \mathrm{ABC}[/math] এ [math]A=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}, B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math] এবং [math]C[/math] কোণের বহিঃস্থ কোণ [math]\theta[/math] হলে [math]2 \sin \theta-\sin C[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]2[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]A=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}, B=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \therefore A+B=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
এখন, [math]\triangle A B C[/math]
 এ [math]A+B+C=\pi \quad \therefore C=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
এখন [math]C[/math]
 এর বহিঃস্থ কোণ, [math]\theta=A+B=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore 2 \sin \theta-\sin C=2 \cdot \sin \frac{\pi}{2}-\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=2.1-1=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #283 (ID: 442748)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018 | Boards:  | Tags: All Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{2}\right)-\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} 1\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{2}[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{2}\right)-\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} 1\right) \\\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)-\left\{1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} 1\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)^{2}-\left\{1-(1)^{2}\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+\frac{1}{2}-(1-1)=\frac{3}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #287 (ID: 442752)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{9}{41}\right)=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{40}{41}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\sec ^{-1}\left(\frac{9}{41}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{40}{41}\right)[/math]
D. [math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{4}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
ধরি, [math]\theta=\frac{1}{2} \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{9}{41}\right) \Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\frac{9}{41}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{9}{41}\\\Rightarrow 41-41 \tan ^{2} \theta=9+9 \tan ^{2} \theta\\\Rightarrow 32=50 \tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{32}{50}=\frac{16}{25}\\\Rightarrow\tan \theta=\frac{4}{5} \quad \therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{4}{5}\right)\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

[math]\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=[/math] কত?

 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4} \therefore \sin \left(\tan ^{-1} 1\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]

Question:
[math]\sin x+\cos x=0[/math] এবং [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে ' x এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

Question #292 (ID: 442757)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin 2 \theta+3 \sin \theta=0[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
B. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin 2 \theta+3 \sin \theta=0 \Rightarrow 2 \cdot \sin \theta \cos \theta+3 \sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta(2 \cos \theta+3)=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin \theta=0[/math]
 অথবা [math]2 \cos \theta+3=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{-3}{2}[/math]
[গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়]

Explanation:

Question #295 (ID: 442760)

Question:
[math]2 \cos \theta-1=0[/math] হলে [math]\theta=\text { ? }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \cos \theta-1=0 \\\therefore \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #304 (ID: 442721)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math] হলে—

i. [math]2 f(x)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]

ii. [math]2 f(x)=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]

iii. [math]2 f(x)=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=2 f(x)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক।বামপক্ষ [math]=2 f(x)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} 
eq[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore[/math]
 (ii) নং সঠিক নয় ।• বামপক্ষ [math]=2 f(x)[/math]
 [math]=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore \text { (iii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক।

Question #305 (ID: 442722)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
বিপরীত বৃত্তীয় ফাংশনের ক্ষেত্রে

i. [math]\cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\}[/math]

ii. [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]

iii. [math]\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
মনে করি, [math]\cos ^{-1} x=A[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos ^{-1} y=\mathrm{B}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos \mathrm{A}=x[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos B=y[/math]
এখন, [math], \cos (A+B)=\cos A \cos B-\sin A \sin B[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\cos (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=\cos \mathrm{A} \cos \mathrm{B}-\sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} \mathrm{~A}} \sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} \mathrm{~B}} \\\Rightarrow \cos (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=x y-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B}=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\} \\\therefore \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) নং সঠিকমনে করি [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta \therefore \tan \theta=x[/math]
আমরা জানি,[math]\begin{array}{l} \cos 2 \theta=\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}} \\\therefore 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (ii) নং সঠিক নয়।মনে করি, [math]\sin ^{-1} x=\theta \therefore \sin \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=\sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)=x \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}-\theta=\cos ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} x \\\therefore \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (iii) নং সঠিক।

Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #306 (ID: 442723)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]4\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin x\right)=5[/math] একটি ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণ।

[math]x[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ [math]4\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin x\right)=5[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 4 \cos ^{2} x+4 \sin x-5=0\\\Rightarrow 4\left(1-\sin ^{2} x\right)+4 \sin x-5=0\\\Rightarrow 4-4 \sin ^{2} x+4 \sin x-5=0\\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} x-4 \sin x+1=0\\\Rightarrow (2 \sin x-1)^{2}=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \sin x-1=0\\\Rightarrow \sin x=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \sin x=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}\\\therefore x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #307 (ID: 442724)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]4\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin x\right)=5[/math] একটি ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণ।

[math]x[/math] এর মান কত ,যখন [math]0

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}, \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{11 \pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
উপরের ব্যাখ্যা হতে প্রা , [math]x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=-\frac{7 \pi}{6}2 \pi[/math]
 তাই গ্রহণযোগ্য নয় ।[math]n=-2[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}-\frac{11 \pi}{6}<0[/math]
 তাই গ্রহণযোগ্য নয়।[math]\therefore 0 [math]x=\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #308 (ID: 442725)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর-দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 3=A, \tan ^{-1} 2=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math] [math]A + B[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 3=\mathrm{A}[/math]
 এবং [math]\tan ^{-1} 2=B[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\therefore \mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B} & =\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 2 \\& =\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{3+2}{1-3.2}[\because 3 \times 2>0] \\& =\pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{-5}\right) \\& =\pi+\tan ^{-1}(-1) \\& =\pi-\tan ^{-1}(1)=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4}\end{aligned}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #309 (ID: 442726)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর-দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 3=A, \tan ^{-1} 2=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math]

নিচের কোন সম্পর্কটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. [math]A-B=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\cot B=2[/math]
গ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}+B[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}-B[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে , [math]\tan ^{-1} 3=A, \tan ^{-1} 2=B[/math]
 এবং [math]B+C=\pi[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}A-B & =\tan ^{-1} 3-\tan ^{-1} 2 \\& =\tan ^{-1} \frac{3-2}{1+3,2}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7} \therefore[/math]
 'ক' অপশনটি সঠিক নয়।[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { } \tan ^{-1} 2=B \\\Rightarrow \tan B=2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot B=\frac{1}{2} \therefore[/math]
 অপশনটি সঠিক নয়।[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { } \tan B=2 \\\Rightarrow \cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-B\right)=2\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}-B=\cot ^{-1} 2 \\\therefore \cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}-B\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 গ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।কিন্তু (ঘ) অপশনটি সঠিক

Explanation:

Question #311 (ID: 442728)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]p=\cos \theta, q=\sin \theta[/math] [math]p-\sqrt{3} q=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]\mathrm{p}=\cos \theta, \mathrm{q}=\sin \theta[/math]
প্রদত্ত সমীকরণ, [math]p-\sqrt{3} q=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin \theta=\cos \theta \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #313 (ID: 442730)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\boldsymbol{\theta}[/math] এর মান কত হবে?

যখন [math]180^{\circ}<\theta<360^{\circ}[/math]

Options:
A. [math]210^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]240^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]300^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]330^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \cot \theta=-\cot \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow \cot \theta=\cot \left(\frac{3 \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\Rightarrow \cot \theta=\cot \frac{10 \pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=300^{\circ}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}= & \frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \cdot \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}} \\\quad & =\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{10}\right)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{aligned}[/math]

Question:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=1-\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}=\frac{2}{3}[/math]

Question #319 (ID: 442718)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
বিপরীত বৃত্তীয় ফাংশনের ক্ষেত্রে-

i. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math] এর পূরক কোণ [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]

ii. [math]\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1}{x}=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math]

iii. [math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}[/math] যখন, [math]x y>1[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\text { } \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\sin ^{-1} \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
এবং [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\cos ^{-1} \cos \frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 এর পূরক কোণ [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
(i) নং সঠিক।
[Image]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{1}{x}[/math]
 [math]=\sec ^{-1} \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore[/math]
(ii) নং সঠিক।মনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\mathrm{A}[/math]
 এবং [math]\tan ^{-1} y=\mathrm{B}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan A=x[/math]
 এবং [math]\tan B=y[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan (A+B)=\frac{\tan A+\tan B}{1-\tan A \tan B}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=\frac{x+y}{1-x y} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}[/math]
 যখন [math]x y \leq 1[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (iii) নং সঠিক নয়।

Explanation:

Question #320 (ID: 442719)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]f_{f}(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] হলে-

[math]\text { i. } f(x)+f\left(\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] [math]\begin{array}{l}\text{ii. } \operatorname{cosec}\{f(x)\}=\frac{1}{x}\\\text{iii. } f(1)=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. i, ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]f(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
বামপক্ষ [math]=f(x)+f\left(\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)[/math]
$\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-x^{2}} \\=\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x\end{array}$[math]=\frac{\pi}{2}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ
[Image]
[math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) নং সঠিক।বামপক্ষ [math]=\operatorname{cosec}\{f(x)\}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\operatorname{cosec} \sin ^{-1} x \\=\operatorname{cosec} \operatorname{cosec}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{x}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore[/math]
 (ii)নং সঠিক।• বামপক্ষ [math]=f(1)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\sin ^{-1} 1 \\=\sin ^{-1} \sin \frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}=[/math]
 ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore[/math]
 (iii) নং সঠিক।

Explanation:

 [যখন [math]x \geq 0][/math]

Question #322 (ID: 442716)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { i. } \tan ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} x=\pi[/math] [math]\text { ii. } \tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-y^{2}\right)\left(1-x^{2}\right)}\right\}[/math]

উপরের তথ্যের প্রেক্ষিতে নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
ধরি, [math]\tan \theta=x \quad \therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan \theta=\cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\cot \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\right)\\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\theta\\\Rightarrow \theta+\cot ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\therefore \tan ^{-1} x+\cot ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2} \quad\left[\because \theta=\tan ^{-1} x\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) নং সঠিক নয়।
[Image]
বামপক্ষ [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}[/math]
= ডানপক্ষ[math]\therefore[/math]
(ii) नए সঠিক।ধরি, [math]\cos ^{-1} x=\mathrm{A}[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos ^{-1} y=\mathrm{B}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos A=x[/math]
 এবং [math]\cos B=y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cos (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=\cos \mathrm{A} \cos \mathrm{B}-\sin \mathrm{A} \sin \mathrm{B} \\\Rightarrow \cos (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=\cos \mathrm{A} \cos \mathrm{B}-\sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} \mathrm{~A}} \sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} \mathrm{~B}} \\\Rightarrow\cos (\mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B})=x y-\sqrt{1-x^{2}} \sqrt{1-y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{A}+\mathrm{B}=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-x^{2}\right)\left(1-y^{2}\right)}\right\} \\\therefore \cos ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} y=\cos ^{-1}\left\{x y-\sqrt{\left(1-y^{2}\right)\left(1-x^{2}\right)}\right\}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (iii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক ।

Explanation:

Question #323 (ID: 442717)
