Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
বিপরীত ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশনের জন্য-

i. [math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এর ডোমেন [math][ -1, 1][/math]

ii. [math]\cos ^{-1} x[/math] এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]

iii. [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math] একটি অনুপাত

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. ii ও iii
গ. i ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]x \in[-1,1][/math]
 এর জন্য [math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 ফাংশনটি সংজ্ঞায়িত[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর ডোমেন [math][−1, 1][/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) নং সঠিক।[math]x \in[-1,1][/math]
 এর জন্য [math]\cos ^{-1} x \in[0, \pi][/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (ii) নং সঠিক।[math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 প্রতীকটি এমন একটি কোণ নির্দেশ করে, যার [math]\text { tangent }[/math]
 অনুপাত x এর সমান। তাই, [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 একটি কোণ হবে,অনুপাত নয় ।[math]\therefore[/math]
 (iii)নং সঠিক নয় ।

Explanation:

Question:
[math]f(\theta)=\cos \theta, f(\theta)=f(\alpha)[/math] এবং [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \alpha[/math]
B. [math]n \pi \pm \alpha[/math]
C. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \alpha[/math]
D. [math]n \pi-(-1)^{n} \alpha[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:

 এবং [math]n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore f(\theta)=f(\alpha) \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \alpha \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \alpha ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Question #326 (ID: 442713)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta \cos ^{2} \theta=-1[/math] হলে নিচের কোনটি সত্য ?

Options:
A. [math]\theta=n \pi[/math]
B. [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]\theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta \cos ^{2} \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)=-1 \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta-\sin ^{3} \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=-1 \\\therefore \theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #327 (ID: 442714)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board

Question:
[math]-2\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=1[/math] এর সমাধান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}-2\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow -2 \cos 2 x=1 \quad\left[\because \cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x=\cos 2 x\right] \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow 2 x=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=\mathbf{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; \mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #328 (ID: 442715)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\theta[/math] হলে-

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2}\\\text{ii. } \cot \theta=-\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\\\text{iii. } \sin \theta=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{3}\end{array}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. ii ও iii
C. i ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\theta[/math]
 [math]•[/math]
 [math]2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2} \therefore[/math]
 (i)নং সঠিক ।[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \theta=2 \tan ^{-1} \dot{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \sqrt{2}}{1-(\sqrt{2})^{2}}\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}} ; x>1\right] \\\Rightarrow \theta=\pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 \cdot \sqrt{2}}{1}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \theta=\pi-\tan ^{-1} 2 \sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \pi-2 \sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=-2 \sqrt{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot \theta=-\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}} \therefore[/math]
 (ii) নং সঠিক।[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}\\\Rightarrow \theta=2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}\\\Rightarrow \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \sqrt{2}}{1+(\sqrt{2})^{2}}\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right]\\\therefore \sin \theta=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (iii) নং সঠিক ।

Explanation:

Question #329 (ID: 442697)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে পাশের ত্রিভুজ হতে পাই,[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]
এবং [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{5}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]2 \cos \theta+1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{5 \pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \cos \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=-\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2}-1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\dot{n} \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2}-1=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{n}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\theta=-(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in Z[/math] হবে যখন-

Options:
A. [math]\cos \theta=0[/math]
B. [math]\sin \theta=0[/math]
C. [math]\cos \theta=1[/math]
D. [math]\sin \theta=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধানের ক্ষেত্রে-[math]\cos \theta=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in Z[/math]
 [math]\sin \theta=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\mathbf{n} \pi ; \mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
এবং [math]\sin \theta=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=(4 \mathrm{n}+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]

Question #333 (ID: 442701)

Question:
[math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] যদি-

Options:
A. [math]\cot \theta=0[/math]
B. [math]\cos \theta+1=0[/math]
C. [math]\sin \theta=1[/math]
D. [math]\cos \theta=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধানের ক্ষেত্রে-[math]\cot \theta=0[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 ‘ক' অপশনটি সঠিক[math]\cos \theta+1=0[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta=-1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'খ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।[math]\sin \theta=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 গ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।[math]\cos \theta=1[/math]
 হলে, [math]\theta=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'ঘ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।

Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে [math]x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{3}[/math]
D. [math]-\sqrt{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow x=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\\Rightarrow x=\cot \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\\Rightarrow x=\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} \sqrt{3}\right) \\\therefore x=\sqrt{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]3 \sec ^{-1}(2)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]-\frac{1}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-1[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]3 \sec ^{-1}(2)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 3 \sec ^{-1} \sec \frac{\pi}{3}=\cos ^{-1} x \quad\left[\because \sec \frac{\pi}{3}=2\right] \\\Rightarrow 3 \cdot \frac{\pi}{3}=\cos ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} x=\pi \\\Rightarrow x=\cos \pi \\\therefore x=-1\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+x^{2}}{2 x}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
B. [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} x[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+x^{2}}{2 x}\right) \\= & \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right)\left[\because \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}\right] \\= & \frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} x\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right] \\= & \tan ^{-1} x\end{aligned}[/math]

Question #339 (ID: 442707)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot \theta \cdot \cot 2 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 n \pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
E. No correct answer


Correct Answer: .

Solution:
[math]\cot \theta \cdot \cot 2 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta} \cdot \frac{\cos 2 \theta}{\sin 2 \theta}=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos 2 \theta=\sin \theta \sin 2 \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos 2 \theta-\sin \theta \sin 2 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \cos (\theta+2 \theta)=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 3 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow 3 \theta=(2 n+1{}) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #340 (ID: 442708)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board

Question:
[math]\cot 2 x \cot x=1[/math] হলে [math]x=1[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math](2 n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot 2 x \cot x=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\cos 2 x}{\sin 2 x} \cdot \frac{\cos x}{\sin x}=1 \\\Rightarrow\cos 2 x \cos x=\sin 2 x \sin x \\\Rightarrow\cos 2 x \cos x-\sin 2 x \sin x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos (2 x+x)=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos 3 x=0 \\\Rightarrow 3 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6} ; \mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [ উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+(\sqrt{3})^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

 [math]n=0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]

Question #349 (ID: 442690)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের কোনটি [math]\sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান?

Options:
A. [math]2 x \sqrt{x^{2}-1}[/math]
B. [math]2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sin \left(2 \sin ^{-1} x\right) \\= & \sin \left\{\sin ^{-1}\left(2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)\right\} \\= & 2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #350 (ID: 442691)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{5}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{2}}{1+\frac{1}{4}}\right) \quad\left[\because 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right][/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\frac{5}{4}}=\frac{4}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #351 (ID: 442681)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot ^{-1} 3=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
B. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\text { - } \cot ^{-1} 3=\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 ক' অপশন সঠিক নয়।[math]\text { - } \cot ^{-1} 3=\cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
'খ' অপশন সঠিক নয় ।[math]\cot ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{9}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{8}{9}}=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{8}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot ^{-1} 3=\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'গ' অপশন সঠিক নয় ।[math]\cot ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}} \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \times \frac{9}{10}\right) \\=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot ^{-1} 3=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'ঘ' অপশন সঠিক

Explanation:

Question #352 (ID: 442682)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]
E. No correct answer


Correct Answer: .

Solution:
[math]\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}[\because 2 \times 3>1][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{-5}\right) \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1}(-1) \\=\pi-\tan ^{-1}(1) \\=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3=\frac{\pi}{4}+\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\pi[/math]


Explanation:

Question #353 (ID: 442683)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\theta[/math] হলে [math]\sin \theta[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]2 x[/math]
D. [math]2 x \sqrt{1-x^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \\\Rightarrow\frac{\pi}{2}=\theta \quad\left[\because \sin ^{-1} x+\cos ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}\right]\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \frac{\pi}{2}[[/math]
 উভয় পক্ষে [math]\sin[/math]
 নিয়ে][math]\therefore \sin \theta=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #354 (ID: 442684)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right) \\= & 1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right) \quad\left[\because \sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta=1\right] \\= & 1-\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2} \\= & 1-\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2} \\= & 1-\frac{1}{4} \\= & \frac{4-1}{4}=\frac{3}{4}\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #355 (ID: 442685)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \tan ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right) \\= & \tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\right) \\= & \sec ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-1 \quad\left[\because \sec ^{2} \theta-\tan ^{2} \theta=1\right] \\= & \left\{\sec \left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\}^{2}-1\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}-1 \\=\frac{4}{3}-1 \\=\frac{4-3}{3} \\=\frac{1}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #356 (ID: 442686)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{3}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right) \\= & \sec ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-1 \quad\left[\because \sec ^{2} \theta-\tan ^{2} \theta=1\right] \\= & \left\{\sec \left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2}-1 \\= & \left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}-1 \\= & \frac{1}{4}-1 \\= & \frac{1-4}{4} \\= & -\frac{3}{4}\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #357 (ID: 442687)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}\right)=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{8}{15}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{4}{7}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{5}}{1-\frac{1}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{5}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\frac{\frac{5+3}{15}}{\frac{15-1}{15}} \\=\frac{8}{15} \times \frac{15}{14} \\=\frac{4}{7}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #358 (ID: 442688)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]5[/math]
B. [math]7[/math]
C. [math]11[/math]
D. [math]15[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)+1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & 2+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}^{2}+\left\{\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}^{2} \\= & 2+2^{2}+3^{2} \\= & 2+4+9 \\= & 15\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\cot k=\frac{1}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\cot \tan ^{-1} \sec \sin ^{-1} \cot k[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cot k=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\cot \tan ^{-1} \sec \sin ^{-1} \cot =\cot \tan ^{-1} \sec \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow\cot \tan ^{-1} \sec \sec ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}=\cot \tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow\cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #361 (ID: 442662)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\theta[/math] হলে-

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } \quad \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2}\\\text{ii. } \cot \theta=-\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } \sin \theta=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{3}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. ii ও iii
C. i ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{(i) } 2 \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \sqrt{2}=\frac{\theta}{2} \therefore \tan \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{2}\\\text{(ii) } \cot \theta=\frac{1}{\tan \theta}\\\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\frac{2 \tan \frac{\theta}{2}}{1-\tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}}=\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{2 \tan \frac{\theta}{2}}=-\frac{1}{2 \sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\text { (iii) } \sin \theta=\frac{2 \tan \frac{\theta}{2}}{1+\tan \frac{\theta}{2}}=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{1+2}=\frac{2 \sqrt{2}}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii

(ii) সঠিক নয়,[math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]

বিপরীত ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশনের জন্য—

i. [math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এর ডোমেন [math][ - 1, 1][/math]

ii. [math]\cos ^{-1} x i[/math] এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]

iii. [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math] একটি অনুপাত

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. ii ও iii
গ. i ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii
ঙ. No correct answer


Correct Answer: .

Solution:
প্রশ্নে সঠিক উত্তর নেই। শুধুমাত্র (i) নং তথ্যটি সঠিক।(ii) নং সঠিক নয়। কারণ, শুধুমাত্র [math]\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]
(iii) নং সঠিক নয়। কারণ, [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 একটি কোণ নির্দেশ করে ।

Question #369 (ID: 442670)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]4\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin x\right)=5[/math] একটি ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণ।

x এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}4\left(\cos ^{2} x+\sin x\right)=5 \\\Rightarrow 4-4 \sin ^{2} x+4 \sin x=5 \\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} x-4 \sin x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \sin x-1)^{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin x=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore x=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]p \doteq \cos \theta, q=\sin \theta[/math] [math]\sec \theta[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-q^{2}}}[/math]
খ. [math]\sqrt{1-q^{2}}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{q^{2}-1}}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\sqrt{q^{2}-1}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sec \theta=\frac{1}{\cos \theta}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\sin ^{2} \theta}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-q^{2}}}[/math]

Question #372 (ID: 442673)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]p = \cos \theta, q=\sin \theta[/math] [math]p-\sqrt{3} q=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{\mathbb { Z }}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}p-\sqrt{3} q=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos \theta-\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin \theta=\cos \theta \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]\tan ^{-1} 3=A, \tan ^{-1} 2,=\mathrm{B}[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math]

নিচের কোন সম্পর্কটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. [math]A-B=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\cot B=2[/math]
গ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}+B[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}-B[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{B}+\cot ^{-1} 2=\tan ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore \cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}-\mathrm{B}\end{array}[/math]

 এর ডোমেন [math][-1,1][/math]

Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এর মুখ্যমানের সীমা নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\left(-\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]
খ. [math]\left[-\frac{{\pi}}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]\left[-\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2}\right] \text {. }[/math]

 [math]\therefore[/math]
 বিপরীত সাইন ফাংশনের মুখ্যমানের সীমা [math]\left[-\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]

Question #377 (ID: 442678)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোন সম্পর্কটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1-x^{2}}[/math]
খ. [math]3 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x^{3}-3 x}{1-3 x^{2}}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \cos ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1}\left(1-2 x^{2}\right)[/math]
ঘ. [math]3 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1}\left(3 x-4 x^{3}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
মনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta \quad \therefore \tan \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 \tan \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow2 \theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}} \\\therefore 2 \tan ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'ক' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।• মনে করি, [math]\tan ^{-1} x=\theta \quad \therefore \tan \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\tan 3 \theta=\frac{3 \tan \theta-\tan ^{3} \theta}{1-3 \tan ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan 3 \theta=\frac{3 x-x^{3}}{1-3 x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 3 \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3 x-x^{3}}{1-3 x^{2}} \\\therefore 3 \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3 x-x^{3}}{1-3 x^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 'খ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।মনে করি, [math]\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \quad \therefore \cos \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\cos 2 \theta=2 \cos ^{2} \theta-1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\cos 2 \theta=2 x^{2}-1 \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=\cos ^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}-1\right) \\\therefore 2 \cos ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}-1\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 ‘গ' অপশনটি সঠিক নয় ।• মনে করি, [math]\sin ^{-1} x=\theta \quad \therefore \sin \theta=x[/math]
এখন, [math]\sin 3 \theta=3 \sin \theta-4 \sin ^{3} \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\sin 3 \theta=3 x-4 x^{3} \\\Rightarrow3 \theta=\sin ^{-1}\left(3 x-4 x^{3}\right) \\\therefore 3 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1}\left(3 x-4 x^{3}\right)\end{array}[/math]
$$'ঘ' অপশনটি সঠিক।

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)-\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]5[/math]
B. [math]15[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{5}-\sqrt{10}[/math]
D. [math]-5[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)-\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)-1-\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\= & \left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}^{2}-\left\{\cot \left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\}^{2} \\= & 2^{2}-3^{3} \\= & 4-9 \\= & -5\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]2 \cos \theta+1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{5 \pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \cos \theta+1=0 \\\quad \Rightarrow 2 \cos \theta=-1 \\\quad \Rightarrow \cos \theta=-\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\quad \therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3},{n} \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta \cos ^{2} \theta=-1[/math] হলে নিচের কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
A. [math]\theta=n \pi[/math]
B. [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]\theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{3} \theta+\sin \theta \cdot \cos ^{2} \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta\left(\sin ^{2} \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta\right)=-1 \\\therefore \sin \theta=-1 \\\therefore \theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Question #384 (ID: 442650)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board

Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে [math]x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{3}[/math]
D. [math]-\sqrt{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{6}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{3} \\\therefore x=\tan \frac{\pi}{3}=\sqrt{3}\end{array}[/math]

[math]-2\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=1[/math] এর সমাধান নিচের কোনটি?

খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]

Question #386 (ID: 442652)

Question:
[math]\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{3}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)=\sec ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-1 \\\Rightarrow \left\{\sec \left(\sec ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2}-1=\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}-1=\frac{1}{4}-1=\frac{-3}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Question #387 (ID: 442653)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot ^{-1} 3=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
B. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{10}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{c}\cot ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cdot \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\frac{1}{9}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{3} \cdot \frac{9}{10}\right)=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]

Question #389 (ID: 442655)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{21}}{5}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\pi[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]2 \pi[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{21}}{5} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{2}{5} \sqrt{1-\frac{21}{25}}+\frac{\sqrt{21}}{5} \sqrt{1-\frac{4}{25}}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{2}{5} \times \frac{2}{5}+\frac{\sqrt{21}}{5} \times \frac{\sqrt{21}}{5}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{4}{25}+\frac{21}{25}\right)=\sin ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]3 \sec ^{-1}(2)=\cos ^{-1} x[/math] হলে x এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]-\frac{1}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-1[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}3 \sec ^{-1} 2=\cos ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow 3 \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}=\cos ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1}\left(4 \times \frac{1}{8}-3 \times \frac{1}{2}\right)=\cos ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2}=x \quad \therefore x=-1\end{array}[/math]

Options:
A. [math]5[/math]
B. [math]15[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{5}-\sqrt{10}[/math]
D. [math]-5[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)-\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\\Rightarrow \left\{1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}-\left\{1+\cot ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)\right\} \\\Rightarrow 1+2^{2}-1-3^{2}=4-9 \\\Rightarrow-5\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2}-1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi+{ }^{\prime}(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=1 \\\Rightarrow \sin \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \frac{\theta}{2}=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{\pi}{3} \cos \theta+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=1 \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=1 \\\therefore \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Question #394 (ID: 442660)

Question:
[math]\cot \theta \cdot \cot 2 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান—

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2 n \pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot \theta \cdot \cot 2 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta} \cdot \frac{\cos 2 \theta}{\sin 2 \theta}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta \cdot \cos \theta=\sin 2 \theta \cdot \sin \theta \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta \cdot \cos \theta-\sin 2 \theta \cdot \sin \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos (2 \theta+\theta)=0 \Rightarrow \cos 3 \theta=0 \\\therefore 3 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]

B. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

C. [math]\frac{8}{15}[/math]

Correct Answer: ঘ

Question #403 (ID: 442641)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board

Question:
[math]\cot 2 x \cot x=1[/math] হলে [math]x=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math](2 n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\cos 2 x}{\sin 2 x}, \frac{\cos x}{\sin x}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x \cos x=\sin 2 x \sin x \\\Rightarrow \cos 2 x \cos x-\sin 2 x \sin x=0 \\\Rightarrow\cos (2 x+x)=0 \\\Rightarrow 3 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

B. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]

Question #410 (ID: 442619)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board

Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})=\tan x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] [math]g(y)+g\left(\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]\pi[/math]
খ. [math]2\pi[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)=\cos ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}& g(x)=\sin ^{-1} x \\\therefore & g(y)=\sin ^{-1} y\end{aligned}[/math]
এবং [math]g\left(\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)=\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\cos ^{-1} y[/math]
 [চিত্র হতে ][math]\begin{aligned}\therefore g(y)+g\left(\sqrt{1-y^{2}}\right) & =\sin ^{-1} y+\cos ^{-1} y \\& =\frac{\pi}{2}\end{aligned}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #411 (ID: 442620)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})=\tan x[/math] এবং [math]g(x)=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] [math]f(x) f(2 x)=1[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in z[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in z[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x})=\tan \mathrm{x} \\\therefore \mathrm{f}(2 \mathrm{x})=\tan 2 \mathrm{x} \\\therefore \mathrm{f}(\mathrm{x}) \mathrm{f}(2 \mathrm{x})=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \mathrm{x} \tan 2 \mathrm{x}=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \mathrm{x} \frac{2 \tan \mathrm{x}}{1-\tan ^{2} \mathrm{x}}=1 \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \mathrm{x}=1-\tan ^{2} \mathrm{x} \\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \mathrm{x}=1 \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \mathrm{x}=\frac{1}{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan \mathrm{x}= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow \tan \mathrm{x}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} ; \mathbf{n} \in \mathbf{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #412 (ID: 442621)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]\cot \theta=k[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\alpha[/math] [math]k=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\alpha=[/math] কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে,[math]\cot \theta=k[/math]
 এবং [math]k=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \cot \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \\\Rightarrow\cot \theta=\cot \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow n \pi+\alpha=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} \quad[\because \theta=n \pi+\alpha] \\\therefore \alpha=\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #413 (ID: 442622)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\cot \theta=k[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\alpha[/math] [math]k=1[/math] এবং [math]\frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]k=1[/math]
, হলে [math]\cot \theta=1=\cot \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
 [math]n=0[/math]
, হলে [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
, হলে [math]=\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]n=2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে, [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #414 (ID: 442623)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\mathbf{x}=\sin \cos ^{-1} \mathbf{y}[/math] হলে [math]x^{2}+y^{2}[/math] এর মান হবে—

Options:
A. [math]\pi[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]-1[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l} x=\sin \cos ^{-1} y \\=\sin \sin ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \\\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+x^{2}}{2 x}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
B. [math]\tan ^{-1} \mathbf{x}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{2^{2}} \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \mathrm{x}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}\left(\frac{1+x^{2}}{2 x}\right) \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right)=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3=\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2.3} \\\Rightarrow\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{1-6}=\frac{\pi}{4}+\tan ^{-1}(-1)=\frac{\pi}{4}-\frac{\pi}{4}=0\end{array}[/math]

Question #420 (ID: 442629)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \sin 2 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান—

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi-(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}-(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{12}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{12}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \sin 2 \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow 2 \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} \therefore \theta=\frac{n \pi}{2}+(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{12}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #421 (ID: 442607)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot ^{-1} p=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{3}{2}[/math] হলে [math]\mathrm{p}=\mathrm{?}[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{-1} p=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{3}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cot ^{-1} p=\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2} \\\Rightarrow p=\cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2} \\\therefore p=\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{3}{2}=\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]

Question #422 (ID: 442608)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 3 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{12}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 n \pi}{12}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan 3 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \tan 3 \theta=\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \text{.}\\\Rightarrow 3 \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in Z\\\therefore \theta=\frac{n \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{12} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Question #423 (ID: 442609)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \theta+1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math](4 n+1)) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math](8 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math](8 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in Z \\\therefore \theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #424 (ID: 442610)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{2} \sin \theta+1=0[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi+(-1)^{n} \cdot \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{4} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঙ. No correct answer


Correct Answer: .

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sqrt{2} \sin \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin \theta=-1 \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\\Rightarrow \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in Z \\\therefore \theta=n \pi-(-1)^{n}\left(\frac{\pi}{4}\right) ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]n[/math] একটি পূর্ণসংখ্যা হলে [math]\cos 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{2}{3} n \pi-\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{2}{3} \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]

Question #427 (ID: 442613)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]-\pi \leq \mathrm{x} \leq \pi[/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]\sin x=-\frac{1}{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সা সমাধান-

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin x=-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin x=\sin \left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right) \\\Rightarrow x=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left(-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=n \pi-(-1)^{n} \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in Z[/math]
এখন, [math]n = 0[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]n=1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=\frac{7 \pi}{6}[/math]
 যা অগ্রহণযোগ্য।[math]n=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]x=-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
অতএব, [math]-\pi \leq x \leq \pi[/math]
 ব্যবধিতে [math]x[/math]
 এর মান: [math]-\frac{\pi}{6},-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #428 (ID: 442614)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\sec \theta=-2[/math] এবং [math]\frac{\pi}{2}<\theta<\pi[/math] হয়, তবে [math]\boldsymbol{\theta}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]-\pi[/math]
D. [math]\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sec \theta=-2[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sec \theta=\sec \left(\frac{2 \pi}{3}\right) \\\therefore \theta \leq 2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}, n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
যেহেতু [math]\frac{\pi}{2}<\theta<\pi[/math]
 সুতরাং [math]\theta=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 হবে ।

Question #429 (ID: 442615)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে [math]\boldsymbol{\theta}[/math] এর মান কত? [math](0<\theta<2 \pi)[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{6},[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}\\\Rightarrow \frac{1+\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}\\\Rightarrow 1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \sin \theta-\cos \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta-\frac{1}{2} \cos \theta=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয় পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{(\sqrt{3})^{2}+(-1)^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{6}-\cos \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \sin \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow\theta-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{\pi}{6} \\\therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #430 (ID: 442616)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে এর [math]\boldsymbol{\theta}[/math] মান-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{\pi}{4} \cos \theta+\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \sin \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi ; n \in Z \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #431 (ID: 442617)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
বিপরীত ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশনের ক্ষেত্রে

i. [math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math] এর ডোমেন [math][ -1, 1][/math]

ii. [math]\cos ^{-1} x[/math] এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]

iii. [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math] এর ডোমেন [math](-\infty, \infty)[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]•[/math]
 [math]x \in[-1,1][/math]
-এর জন্য [math]\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 ফাংশনটি সংজ্ঞায়িত[math]\therefore \sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর ডোমেন [math][ -1, 1][/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) নং সঠিক।[math]•[/math]
 [math]x \in[-1,1][/math]
 এর জন্য [math]\cos ^{-1} x \in[0, \pi][/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর রেঞ্জ [math][0, \pi][/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 (ii) নং সঠিক।[math]x[/math]
 এর সকল বাস্তব মান [math]\mathbb{R}[/math]
 এর জন্য [math]\tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 ফাংশনটি সংজ্ঞায়িত।[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর ডোমেন $$\mathbb{R} বা, [math](-\infty, \infty)[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
(iii) নং সঠিক

Explanation:

Question #432 (ID: 442618)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
অবান্তর মূল ত্রিকোণমিতিক সমীকরণকে

i. সিদ্ধ করে না

ii. বর্গ করলে পাওয়া যায়

iii. বর্গমূল করলে পাওয়া যায়

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
অবান্তর মূল: যে সমস্ত মূল সমীকরণকে সিদ্ধ করে না তাদেরকে অবান্তর মূল বলে। কোনো সমীকরণ সমাধান করার সময় যদি উভয় পক্ষকে বর্গ করা হয় তবে সাধারণত এক বা একাধিক মূল পাওয়া যায়, যাদের দ্বারা সমীকরণটি সিদ্ধ হয় না। যেমন:-[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sqrt{2}-\cos \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta=2-2 \sqrt{2} \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta[/math]
 [বর্গ করে ][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 1-\cos ^{2} \theta-2+2 \sqrt{2} \cos \theta-\cos ^{2} \theta=0\\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} \theta-2 \sqrt{2} \cos \theta+1=0\\\Rightarrow (\sqrt{2} \cos \theta-1)^{2}=0\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \cos \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\\\Rightarrow \cos \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{4}\\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}: n \in Z\end{array}[/math]
কিন্তু [math]\theta=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 মূল দ্বারা সমীকরণটি সিদ্ধ নয়।অর্থাৎ, [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 একটি অবান্তর মূল ।উল্লেখ্য, সমীকরণটিকে বর্গ করায় এই অবান্তর মূলটি এসেছে। নির্দিষ্ট সমীকরণেরসাথে আরও একটি সমীকরণ [math]\cos \theta-\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 এর সমাধান [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
অন্তর্ভুক্ত হয়েছে যা [math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 সমীকরণের জন্যে অবান্তর মূল।[math]\therefore[/math]
 (i) ও (ii) নং সঠিক, (iii) নং সঠিক নয় ।

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\right)+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{-1}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{-1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে
[Image]
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{4} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+x}{1-2 x}=\tan ^{-1}(1) \\\Rightarrow \frac{2+x}{1-2 x}=1 \\\Rightarrow 2+x=1-2 x \\\Rightarrow 3 x=-1 \\\therefore x=-\frac{1}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Question #435 (ID: 442604)

Question:
[math]\tan 5 \theta \cdot \tan 4 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
খ. [math](2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
গ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan 5 \theta \cdot \tan 4 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 5 \theta}{\cos 5 \theta} \cdot \frac{\sin 4 \theta}{\cos 4 \theta}=1\\\Rightarrow \sin 5 \theta \cdot \sin 4 \theta=\cos 5 \theta \cdot \cos 4 \theta\\\Rightarrow \cos 5 \theta \cdot \cos 4 \theta-\sin 5 \theta^{\prime} \sin 4 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow \cos (5 \theta+4 \theta)=0\\\Rightarrow \cos 9 \theta=0\\\Rightarrow 9 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{18} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Question #436 (ID: 442605)

Question:
[math]\sec x=\sec (x+\pi)[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান:

Options:
ক. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi+\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec x=\sec (x+\pi) \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos x}=\frac{1}{\cos (x+\pi)} \\\Rightarrow \cos x=\cos (x+\pi) \\\Rightarrow \cos x=-\cos x \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos x=0 \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]n[/math] একটি পূর্ণসংখ্যা হলে [math]2 \cos 2 \theta-1=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n-\pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \cos 2 \theta-1=0\\\Rightarrow2 \cos 2 \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta=\frac{1}{2}\\\Rightarrow\cos 2 \dot{\theta}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}\\\Rightarrow2 \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}\\\therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Question #439 (ID: 442600)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \left(\cos ^{-1} x\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan \left(\cos ^{-1} x\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\right)=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}\right) \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow 2 x=\sqrt{5} \cdot \sqrt{1-x^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 4 x^{2}=5\left(1-x^{2}\right) \\\Rightarrow 4 x^{2}=5-5 x^{2} \\\Rightarrow 9 x^{2}=5 \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\frac{5}{9} \\\therefore x=\frac{\sqrt{5}}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{-1} x[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে,
[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1} 2[/math]
 এর ক্ষেত্রে;
[Image]
[math]\therefore \tan ^{-1} 2=\sin ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]


Explanation:

A. [math]\mathbf{X}[/math]
B. [math]x^{2}[/math]
C. [math]x^{3}[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x \\= & \sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\= & \sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\= & \sin \sin ^{-1} x \\= & x\end{aligned}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]

A. [math]-\pi[/math]
B. [math]\pi[/math]

D. [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Correct Answer: .

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{4}{5} \\= & \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\= & 2 \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\= & 73.74^{\circ}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]=1.287[/math]
 রেডিয়ানকিন্তু প্রদত্ত অপশনগুলোর কোনোটিই [math]73.74^{\circ}[/math]
 বা [math]1.287[/math]

Question:
[math]2 \sin ^{-1} x=\sin ^{-1} y[/math] সমীকরণে [math]x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math] হলে [math]y[/math] এর মান কত?

A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]

Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2=\frac{\pi}{3}=\sin ^{-1} \mathrm{y} \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{y}=\sin \left(\frac{2 \pi}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{y}=\sin \left(\pi-\frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow \mathrm{y}=\sin \frac{\pi}{3} \\\therefore \mathrm{y}=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Question #447 (ID: 442594)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\cot ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math]
 [math]=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}[/math]
 যেহেতু [math]2 \times 3>1][/math]
[ সূত্র [math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}[/math]
 যখন, [math]x y>1, x>0, y>0][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{-5} \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1}(-1) \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Question #448 (ID: 442595)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022, 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board, Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4 }[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math]
 [math]=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}[/math]
 যেহেতু [math]2 \times 3>1][/math]
[ সূত্র [math]\tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y}[/math]
 যখন [math]x y>1, x>0, y>0][/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{-5} \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1}(-1) \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{5}{4} .[/math]
B. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}& \operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}\right) \\= & \operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right) \\= & \left\{\operatorname{cosec}\left(\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right)\right\}^{2} \\= & \left(\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right)^{2}=\frac{5}{4}\end{aligned}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\therefore \sec ^{-1} \sqrt{5}=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]

 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{5-2}{10}}{\frac{10+1}{10}}\right)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{10} \times \frac{10}{11}\right) \\=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{11}\right) \\=\cot ^{-1}\left(\frac{11}{3}\right)\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে [math]\sin 2 \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math](4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math](4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:

 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]

Question #457 (ID: 442575)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin 2 \theta-\cos 2 \theta=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান—

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{8}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin 2 \theta-\cos 2 \theta=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin 2 \theta=\cos 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan 2 \theta=1=\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \therefore 2 \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{8} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #458 (ID: 442576)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos x+2+\sec x=0[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi[/math]
খ. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
গ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cos x+2+\sec x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x+\frac{1}{\cos x}=-2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos ^{2} x+1+2 \cos x=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math](\cos x+1)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos x+1=0 \therefore \cos x=-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 নির্ণেয় সাধারণ সমাধান, [math]x=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]


Explanation:

Question #459 (ID: 442577)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos ^{2} x+2 \sin x=2[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান (যখন [math]\mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z})-[/math]

Options:
A. [math](4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
C. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
D. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos ^{2} x+2 \sin x=2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]1-\sin ^{2} x+2 \sin x=2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin ^{2} x-2 \sin x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math](\sin x-1)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin x=1=\sin \frac{\pi}{2} \therefore x=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]2\left(3 \cos \theta-4 \cos ^{3} \theta\right)=-1[/math] এর সমাধান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2 n \pi}{3} \pm \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 n \pi}{3} \pm \frac{\pi}{18}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]2\left(3 \cos \theta-4 \cos ^{3} \theta\right)=-1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2\left(4 \cos ^{3} \theta-3 \cos \theta\right)=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \cos 3 \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\cos 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} . \quad \therefore 3 \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
অর্থাৎ, নির্ণেয় সমাধান, [math]\theta=\frac{2 n \pi}{3} \pm \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]

Question #464 (ID: 442582)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt[]{3}(0<\theta<\pi)[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{1+\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]1+\cos \theta=\sqrt{3} \sin \theta[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{3} \sin \theta-\cos \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin \theta \cdot \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}-\cos \theta \cdot \frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\sin \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{6}\right)=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta-\frac{\pi}{6}=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \cdot \frac{\pi}{6}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \theta=\mathrm{n} \pi+(-1)^{\mathrm{n}} \frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{\pi}{6} ;[0<\theta<\pi[/math]
 শর্তে ] [math]\theta=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question:
[math]\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{-1}{2}\right)[/math] এর মুখ্যমান কত

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{-2 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{-1}{2}\right) & =\cos ^{-1} \cos \left( \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}\right) \\& = \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{-1}{2}\right)[/math]
 এর মুখ্যমান [math]=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]

Question #466 (ID: 442584)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]-1[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\because \sin ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right) \\=1-\cos ^{2}\left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)-\left\{1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)\right\} \\=1-\left\{\cos \left(\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}\right)\right\}^{2}-1+\left\{\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)\right\}^{2} \\=-\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)^{2} \\=-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{3}{4} \\=\frac{-1+3}{4} \\=\frac{2}{4} \\=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]n[/math] একটি পূর্ণ সংখ্যা হলে [math]\cos 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

খ. [math]\frac{2 \mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]

Question #470 (ID: 442571)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Mymensingh Board | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} p+\tan ^{-1} q=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] হলে-

Options:
A. [math]\mathrm{pq}=1[/math]
B. [math]p+q=0[/math]
C. [math]p+q-p q=1[/math]
D. [math]p+q+p q=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} p+\tan ^{-1} q=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{p+q}{1-p q}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\frac{p+q}{1-p q}=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{p}+\mathrm{q}=1-\mathrm{pq} \therefore \mathrm{p}+\mathrm{q}+\mathrm{pq}=1.[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta[/math] এর বৃহত্তম মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{2}+1[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
কেবলমাত্র [math]\theta=45^{\circ}[/math]
 হলে [math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta[/math]
 এর মান সর্বোচ্চ হবে।তখন [math]\sin 45^{\circ}+\cos 45^{\circ}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=2 \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]

Question #475 (ID: 442567)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{24}{7}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan ^{-1} \frac{7}{24}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\frac{1}{2} \cdot 2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\=\frac{1}{2} \cdot \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{3}{4}}{1+\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{16+9}{16}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{24}{25}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #476 (ID: 442568)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{2 b}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math] হলে x এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]a+b[/math]
B. [math]a-b[/math]
C. [math]\frac{a-b}{1+a b}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 a}{1+a^{2}}+\cot ^{-1} \frac{1-b^{2}}{2 b}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \tan ^{-1} a+\tan ^{-1} \frac{2 b}{1-b^{2}}=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]2 \tan ^{-1} a+2 \tan ^{-1} b=2 \tan ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{a+b}{1-a b}=\tan ^{-1} x, \therefore x=\frac{a+b}{1-a b}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #477 (ID: 442561)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023, 2022 | Boards: Sylhet Board, Dhaka Board | Tags: Sylhet Board, Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]n[/math] একটি পূর্ণ সংখ্যা হলে [math]\cos 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](6 n-1) \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{2 \mathrm{n} \pi}{3}+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{2 n \pi}{3} \pm \frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos 3 \theta=\frac{1}{2} \\\Rightarrow \cos 3 \theta=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow 3 \theta=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3} ; n \in \mathbb{Z} \\\therefore \theta=\frac{2 n \pi}{3} \pm \frac{\pi}{9} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #493 (ID: 442381)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Rangpur Cadet College, Rangpur
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান হলো-

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2 \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \frac{\pi}{3} \cdot \cos \theta+\sin \frac{\pi}{3} \cdot \sin \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=1 \quad \therefore \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sin x+\cos x=0[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathrm{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}, n \in Z[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin x+\cos x=0 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin x}{\cos x}=-1 \Rightarrow \tan x=-\tan \frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow \tan x=\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \\\therefore x=n \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}, n \in Z\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:
