Question #497 (ID: 442385)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Ideal School & College, Motijheel, Dhaka
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan x \tan 3 x=1[/math] হলে [math]x=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math](2 n-1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math](3 n-1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math](4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan x \tan 3 x=1 \\\Rightarrow \frac{\sin x}{\cos x} \cdot \frac{\sin 3 x}{\cos 3 x}=1 \\\Rightarrow \sin x \sin 3 x=\cos x \cos 3 x \\\Rightarrow \cos x \cos 3 x-\sin x \sin 3 x=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos (3 x+x)=0 \\\Rightarrow 4 x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{8}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #498 (ID: 442386)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Abdul Kadir Molla City College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=1[/math] এর সমাধান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
B. [math]x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
C. [math]x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
D. [math]x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8} ; n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos ^{2} x-\sin ^{2} x\right)=1 \Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #499 (ID: 442387)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Viqarunnisa Noon School & College, Dhaka

Question:
[math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে [math]\cos 2 x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] এর সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math](8 n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math](8 n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math](n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
D. [math](n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos 2 x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\Rightarrow \cos 2 x=\cos \frac{\pi}{4} \Rightarrow 2 x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\\\Rightarrow x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8^{*}}=\frac{8 n \pi \pm \pi}{8}=(8 n \pm 1) \frac{\pi}{8} \Rightarrow n \in \mathbb{Z}\end{array}[/math]

Question #500 (ID: 442388)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: National Ideal College, Khilgaon, Dhaka
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos x=\sin x[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos x=\sin x \Rightarrow \tan x=1\\\Rightarrow\tan x=\tan \frac{x}{4}=\tan \left(\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\right) \therefore x=\frac{5 \pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Question #501 (ID: 442389)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Ghatail Cantonment Public School & College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2(\cos x+\sec x)=5[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2(\cos x+\sec x)=5 \\\Rightarrow \cos x+\frac{1}{\cos x}=\frac{5}{2} \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x+2=5 \cos x \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x-5 \cos x+2=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos ^{2} x-4 \cos x-\cos x+2=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x(\cos x-2)-1(\cos x-2)=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \cos x-1)(\cos x-2)=0 \\2 \cos x=1 \\\therefore \cos x=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3}[\because \cos x-2 
eq 0] \\x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #504 (ID: 442392)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Cantonment Public School & College, Rangpur
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি, [math]\tan \theta=\cot \theta[/math] হয়, তবে [math]0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] এর জন্য [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan \theta=\cot \theta\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{1}{\tan \theta} \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\tan \theta=\pm 1 \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\pm \tan \frac{\pi}{4} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{4}\left[\because 0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}\right][/math]


Explanation:

Question #506 (ID: 442394)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Dinajpur Govt. College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{2} x+\cos x=\frac{5}{4}[/math] এর সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]2 \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \pi \pm \frac{n \pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{2} x+\cos x=\frac{5}{4} \\\Rightarrow1-\cos ^{2} x+\cos x=\frac{5}{4} \\\Rightarrow 4-4 \cos ^{2} x+4 \cos x=5 \\\Rightarrow 4 \cos ^{2} x-4 \cos x+1=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \cos x-1)^{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \cos x-1=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos x=\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{\pi}{3} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #507 (ID: 442395)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Noakhali Govt. College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta=3,0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2\left(1-\sin ^{2} \theta\right)+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta=3 \\\Rightarrow 2-2 \sin ^{2} \theta+2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta-3=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin ^{2} \theta-2 \sqrt{2} \sin \theta+1=0 \\\Rightarrow (\sqrt{2} \sin \theta-1)^{2}=0 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2} \sin \theta=1 \Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}=\sin \frac{\pi}{4} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Police Lines School and College, Rangpur

Question:
[math]\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]

Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

[math]\cos \cot ^{-1} \tan \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{3}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \cot ^{-1} \tan \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \cos \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\cos \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}} \\\Rightarrow\cos \cot ^{-1} \frac{3}{\sqrt{7}} \\\Rightarrow\cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{3}{4}=\frac{3}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Question #530 (ID: 442356)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Viqarunnisa Noon School & College, Dhaka
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে [math]x=\text { ? }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}} \\\Rightarrow x=\tan \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right) \\\Rightarrow x=\cot \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\cot \cot ^{-1} \frac{2}{1} \\\therefore x=2\end{array}[/math]
[Image]


Explanation:

Question #534 (ID: 442355)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Ideal School & College, Motijheel, Dhaka
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \frac{13}{36}[/math]
B. [math]3 \frac{11}{13}[/math]
C. [math]25 \frac{7}{9}[/math]
D. [math]5 \frac{12}{13}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec ^{2}\left(\cot ^{-1} 3\right)+\operatorname{cosec}^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right) \\\Rightarrow \left(\sec \sec ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{10}}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\operatorname{cosec} \operatorname{cosec}^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right)^{2}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \left(\frac{\sqrt{10}}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\right)^{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{10}{9}+\frac{5}{4} \\\Rightarrow \frac{85}{36}=2 \frac{13}{36}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #535 (ID: 442350)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022, 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: IDEAL COLLEGE, DHANMONDI, Ideal School & College, Motijheel, Dhaka
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]4\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\pi[/math]
D. [math]4 \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}4\left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\cot ^{-1} 3\right) \\\Rightarrow 4\left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow 4 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}=4 \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{6}{6}}{\frac{6-1}{6}} \\\Rightarrow 4 \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{5}{6} \times \frac{6}{5}\right)=4 \tan ^{-1}(1)=4 \times \frac{\pi}{4}=\pi\end{array}[/math]
[Image]


Explanation:

Question #538 (ID: 442353)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Pabna Cadet College
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\cot ^{-1} 3=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}+\cot ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{5}{6}}{\frac{5}{6}}=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4} \text { (Ans.) }\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #542 (ID: 442336)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] হলে x এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=\sin ^{-1} x\\\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\right) \therefore x=\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]

Question #543 (ID: 442337)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})=\sin ^{-1} x[/math] হলে-

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } f(1)=\frac{\pi}{2}\\\text{ii. } f\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1} x\end{array}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } f(x)-f(y)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] এর জন্য হবে [math]x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}-y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}=1[/math] হবে

উপরের বাক্যগুলির মধ্যে কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. ii ও iii
C. i ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} x-\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left(x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}-y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2} \\\therefore x \sqrt{1-y^{2}}-y \sqrt{1-x^{2}}=\sin \frac{\pi}{2}=1\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #545 (ID: 442339)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]f(x)=\tan ^{-1} x[/math] [math]f\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)-f\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{11}[/math]
গ. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{7}{11}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{7}{9}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l} f\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)-f\left(\frac{1}{5}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{5} \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{5}}{1+\frac{1}{2}, \frac{1}{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{11}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos ^{-1}\left(-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)[/math] এর মুখ্যমান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

Question #556 (ID: 442315)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math] হলে [math]\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{9}{25}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{7}{25}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{16}{25}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{6}{25}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\theta=\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [পার্শ্বের চিত্র থেকে]
[Image]
[math]\therefore \tan \theta=\frac{3}{4}[/math]
এখন [math]\frac{1-\tan ^{2} \theta}{1+\tan ^{2} \theta}=\frac{1-\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{2}}{1+\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^{2}}=\frac{\frac{16-9}{16}}{\frac{16+9}{16}}=\frac{7}{25}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #557 (ID: 442316)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান -

Options:
ক. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
খ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1\\\Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \cdot \tan \theta=1 \text{.}\\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta+\tan ^{2} \theta=1\\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=\frac{1}{3}\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}} \Rightarrow \tan \theta= \pm \tan \frac{\pi}{6}\\\Rightarrow\tan \theta=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #568 (ID: 442327)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\cot ^{2} \theta+\operatorname{cosec} \theta-5=0[/math] হয়, তবে [math]0<\theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] এর জন্য [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে—

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } 30^{\circ}\\\text{ii. } 60^{\circ}\\\text{iii. } \operatorname{cosec}^{-1}(3)\end{array}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i
B. ii
C. iii
D. i ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot ^{2} \theta+\operatorname{cosec} \theta-5=0 \\\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta-1+\operatorname{cosec} \theta-5=0 \\\Rightarrow \operatorname{cosec}^{2} \theta+3 \operatorname{cosec} \theta-2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta-6=0 \\\Rightarrow (\operatorname{cosec} \theta+3)(\operatorname{cosec} \theta-2)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta=-3[/math]
 অথবা [math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta=2[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \theta=\operatorname{cosec}^{-1}(-3) \cdot \quad \therefore \sin \theta=\frac{1}{2}=\sin \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
সুতরাং [math]\theta=30^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #569 (ID: 442328)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 2=A, \tan ^{-1} 3=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math] [math]\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{B}[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3=\tan ^{-\frac{2+3}{1-6}}=\tan ^{-1}(-1) \\\Rightarrow \pi-\tan ^{-1}\left(\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\pi-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{3 \pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #570 (ID: 442329)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 2=A, \tan ^{-1} 3=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math] [math]C[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 2=A, \tan ^{-1} 3=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math]

নিচের কোন সম্পর্কটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}+\mathrm{A}[/math]
খ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 3=\frac{\pi}{2}-B[/math]
গ. [math]\cot ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cot B=3[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot ^{-1} 3+B=\cot ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 3=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question #572 (ID: 442331)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Nexes Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\tan ^{-1} 2=A, \tan ^{-1} 3=B[/math] এবং [math]A+B+C=\pi[/math]

উদ্দীপকের ক্ষেত্রে—

i. [math]\tan ^{-1} 2[/math] এর পূরক কোণ [math]\cot ^{-1} 2[/math]

ii. [math]C[/math] এর পূরক কোণ 4

iii. [math]A – B[/math] এর পূরক কোণ [math]\frac{\pi}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{7}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\text { (i) } \tan ^{-1} 2=\frac{\pi}{2}-\cot ^{-1} 2[/math]
\(ii) যেহেতু [math]C=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 সেহেতু [math]C[/math]
 এর পূরক কোণ [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\text { (iii) } \begin{aligned}& \mathrm{A}-\mathrm{B}=\tan ^{-1} 2-\tan ^{-1} 3 \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2-3}{1+6}\right) \\\Rightarrow & \tan ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{7}\right) \\\Rightarrow & -\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 পূরক কোণ [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}-\left\{-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)\right\}=\frac{\pi}{2}+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{7}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #575 (ID: 442310)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{11}[/math]
B. [math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\cos ^{-1} \frac{2}{\sqrt{5}}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}[/math]

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{4}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}{1-\frac{4}{3} \cdot \frac{1}{2}}\right) \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{8+3}{6}}{\frac{6-4}{6}}\right)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{11}{2}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #576 (ID: 442311)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{2} \theta+2 \sec ^{2} \theta=5[/math] সমীকরণটির সাধারণ সমাধান -

Options:
ক. [math]\theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\theta=\frac{n \pi+\pi}{6}[/math]
গ. [math]2 n \pi[/math]
ঘ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{2} \theta+2 \sec ^{2} \theta=5 \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta+2\left(1+\tan ^{2} \theta\right)-5=0 \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta+2+2 \tan ^{2} \theta-5=0\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta-3=0\\\Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=3 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} \theta=1\\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm 1 \Rightarrow \tan \theta= \pm \tan \frac{\pi}{4}\\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #577 (ID: 442312)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান -

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos \theta+\sqrt{3} \sin \theta=2 \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \cos \theta+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \sin \theta=\frac{2}{2}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+(\sqrt{3})^{2}}=2[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=1 \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)=1 \\\Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{3}=2 n \pi \therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

================================================================================
Question #578 (ID: 442313)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{2} 2 \theta-3 \cos ^{2} \theta=0,(\cos \theta 
eq 0)[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান—

Options:
A. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{2} 2 \theta-3 \cos ^{2} \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow (2 \sin \theta \cos \theta)^{2}-3 \cos ^{2} \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} \theta \cos ^{2} \theta=3 \cos ^{2} \theta \\\Rightarrow 4 \sin ^{2} \theta=3 \Rightarrow \sin ^{2} \theta=\frac{3}{4} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\Rightarrow \sin \theta= \pm \sin \frac{\pi}{3} \\\Rightarrow \sin \theta=\sin \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi+(-1)^{n}\left( \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\right) \\\therefore \theta=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #579 (ID: 442314)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সাধারণ সমাধান

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]
[উভয়পক্ষকে [math]\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 দ্বারা ভাগ করে][math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}+\cos \theta \cos \theta \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \\\therefore \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 n \pi \\\therefore \theta=2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #585 (ID: 442299)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}, \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math] এবং [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math] তিনটি বিপরীত ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশন ।

৩য় ফাংশন [math]=\frac{1}{2^{\sin ^{-1}} x}[/math] হলে [math]x =[/math] কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{5}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
৩য় ফাংশন [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} .2 \tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{2 \cdot \frac{1}{3}}{1+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{\frac{2}{3}}{\frac{10}{9}}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #586 (ID: 442300)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও: 

[math]\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}, \frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math] এবং [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math] তিনটি বিপরীত ত্রিকোণমিতিক ফাংশন । 

১ম ফাংশন – ২য় ফাংশন + ৩য় ফাংশন = কত ?

Options:
ক. ১ম ফাংশন
খ. ২য় ফাংশন
গ. ৩য় ফাংশন
ঘ. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math] ১ম ফাংশন


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
এখানে [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}=\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}-\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}+\frac{1}{2} \sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}=[/math]
 ১ম ফাংশন

Explanation:

Question #591 (ID: 442305)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{3}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\cot ^{-1} 3\right)=\sin \left(\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{2}+\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{3}\right) \\\Rightarrow \sin \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{3}}{1-\frac{1}{2} \cdot \frac{1}{3}}\right)=\sin \tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{3+2}{6}}{\frac{6-1}{6}}\right)=\sin \tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{5} \\\Rightarrow \sin \tan ^{-1} 1=\sin \frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #593 (ID: 442307)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান -

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\operatorname{cosec} \theta+\cot \theta=\sqrt{3} \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sin \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{1+\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=\sqrt{3} \\\Rightarrow \frac{2 \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}{2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2} \cos \frac{\theta}{2}}=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow \frac{\cos \frac{\theta}{2}}{\sin \frac{\theta}{2}}=\sqrt{3} \\\Rightarrow \cot \frac{\theta}{2}=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow\cot \frac{\theta}{2}=\cot \frac{\pi}{6} \\\Rightarrow \frac{\theta}{2}=\frac{\pi}{6} \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #597 (ID: 442274)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{5}}[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{-1} \sin \tan ^{-1} x=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{5}}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \sin \cdot \sin ^{-1} \frac{\mathrm{x}}{\sqrt{1+\mathrm{x}^{2}}}=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{5}}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\cos ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{3}{5}}\\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\\\Rightarrow\frac{x}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\\\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}=\frac{2}{3}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 3 x^{2}=2+2 x^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}=2 \Rightarrow x=\pm \sqrt{2}[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #607 (ID: 442284)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1}(-\cos x)+\sin ^{-1}(\cos 3 x)=[/math] কত ?

Options:
A. [math]-2 x[/math]
B. [math]-3{x}[/math]
C. [math]2 x[/math]
D. [math]3 x[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1}(-\cos x)+\sin ^{-1} \cdot(\cos 3 x) \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{-\cos x \sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} 3 x}+\cos 3 x \sqrt{1-\cos ^{2} x}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\{-\cos x \cdot \sin 3 x+\cos 3 x \sin x\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \sin (x-3 x)=-2 x\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #608 (ID: 442285)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{2} x+\sec ^{2} x=3[/math] হলে সমীকরণটির সমাধান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]x=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\tan ^{2} x+\sec ^{2} x=3 \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x+1+\tan ^{2} x=3 \\\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x=2 \\\Rightarrow \tan x=\pm 1=\pm \tan \frac{\pi}{4}=\tan \left(\pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right) . \\\therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2 \cos x+1=0[/math] হলে [math]x =[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}2 \cos x+1=0 \Rightarrow 2 \cos x=-1 \\\Rightarrow \cos x=-\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3} \\\therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}\end{array}[/math]

Question #622 (ID: 442268)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 4 \theta+\tan 3 \theta=0[/math] এবং [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর সাধারণ সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\mathrm{n} {\pi}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{n \pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\mathrm{n} \pi}{7}[/math]
D. [math](2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\tan 4 \theta+\tan 3 \theta=0\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 4 \theta}{\cos 4 \theta}+\frac{\sin 3 \theta}{\cos 3 \theta}=0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{\sin 4 \theta \cdot \cos 3 \theta+\sin 3 \theta \cdot \cos 4 \theta}{\cos 4 \theta \cdot \cos 3 \theta}=0 \Rightarrow \sin (4 \theta+3 \theta)=0\\\Rightarrow \sin 7 \theta=0 \Rightarrow 7 \theta=n \pi \Rightarrow \theta=\frac{n \pi}{7}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #627 (ID: 442273)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cot x \tan \alpha=1[/math] এবং [math]\mathbf{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math] হলে x এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi+\alpha[/math]
B. [math]2 n \pi-\alpha[/math]
C. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi-\alpha[/math]
D. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\alpha[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\cot x, \tan \alpha=1 \Rightarrow \frac{\cos x}{\sin x} \cdot \frac{\sin \alpha}{\cos \alpha}=1 \\\Rightarrow \sin x \cdot \cos \alpha-\cos x \cdot \sin \alpha=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin (x-\alpha)=0 \\\Rightarrow x-\alpha=n \pi+0 \\\Rightarrow n x=n \pi+\alpha\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #642 (ID: 442251)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের ভিত্তিতে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]\cot \theta=k[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\alpha .[/math] [math]k=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}[/math] হলে [math]\alpha=[/math] কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{k}=\cot \theta[/math]
 [math]\therefore \cot \theta=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\Rightarrow \cot \theta=\cot \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
সুতরাং, [math]\theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 অর্থাৎ, [math]\alpha=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

================================================================================
Question #643 (ID: 442252)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের ভিত্তিতে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]\cot \theta=k[/math] সমীকরণটির সমাধান [math]\theta=n \pi+\alpha .[/math] [math]k=1[/math] এবং [math]\frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\cot \theta=\mathrm{k} \Rightarrow , \cot \theta=1 \quad[\because \mathrm{k}=1][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cot \theta=\cot \frac{\pi}{4} \quad \therefore \theta=n \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm {n}=0[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=0+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\mathrm{n}=2[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=2 \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{9 \pi}{4}[/math]
যেহেতু [math]\frac{\pi}{4}<\theta<2 \pi[/math]
 সেহেতু [math]\theta=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #645 (ID: 442238)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:

[Image]


উপরের চিত্রে—

i. ফাংশনটির লেখ সর্বদাই ১ম বা ২য় চতুর্ভাগে থাকবে

ii. মুখ্যমান [math][0, \pi][/math]

iii. ডোমেন [math][-1,1][/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii

Question #652 (ID: 442218)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]3 \sin ^{-1} x=2 \sin ^{-1} y[/math] সমীকরণে [math]x=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math] হলে [math]y[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]0[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}3 \sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)=2 \sin ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow 3 \cdot \frac{\pi}{3}=2 \sin ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow y=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=1\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #653 (ID: 442219)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{2}[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{-1}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{-2}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \cos \tan ^{-1} x \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \cos \cos ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
এখন [math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{5}{2}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{29}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \sin ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x^{2}}}=\sin ^{-1} \frac{5}{\sqrt{29}}\\\Rightarrow 1+x^{2}=\frac{29}{25} \Rightarrow 25 x^{2}=29-25\\\therefore \mathrm{x}=\pm \frac{2}{5}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #656 (ID: 442222)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোন মানটি [math]\sin x+\sin 2 x+\sin 3 x=0[/math] সমীকরণকে সিদ্ধ করে ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin x+\sin 2 x+\sin 3 x=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin \frac{3 x+x}{2} \cos \frac{3 x-x}{2}+\sin 2 x=0 \\\Rightarrow 2 \sin 2 x \cos x+\sin 2 x=0 \\\Rightarrow \sin 2 x(2 \cos x+1)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\sin 2 x=\theta=\sin 0^{\circ}[/math]
 অথবা [math]2 \cos x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 x=n \pi \Rightarrow \cos x=-\frac{1}{2}=\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{n \pi}{2} \quad \therefore x=2 n \pi \pm \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #657 (ID: 442223)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\left[0,360^{\circ}\right][/math] ব্যবধিতে [math]\sqrt{3} \tan ^{2} \theta+\sqrt{3}=4 \tan \theta[/math] সমীকরণের সমাধান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]135^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]300^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sqrt{3} \tan ^{2} \theta+\sqrt{3}=4 \tan \theta\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{3} \tan ^{2} \theta-4 \tan \theta+\sqrt{3}=0\\\Rightarrow (\tan \theta-\sqrt{3})\left(\tan \theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=0\end{array}[/math]
হয় [math]\tan \theta-\sqrt{3}=0[/math]
 অথবা [math]\tan \theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=60^{\circ}[/math]
 অথবা [math]\theta=30^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #665 (ID: 442231)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)[/math] এর মান নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{7}[/math]
B. [math]5[/math]
C. [math]12[/math]
D. [math]25[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right) \\\quad\Rightarrow 1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\sec ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)-1 \\\quad\Rightarrow 4^{2}+3^{2}=25\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #666 (ID: 442232)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{5}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\sin ^{-1}\left(A+\frac{9}{25}\right)[/math] হলে [math]A[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{9}{25}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{16}{25}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\sin ^{-1} \frac{4}{3}+\sin ^{-1} \frac{3}{5} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{4}{5} \sqrt{1-\frac{3^{2}}{5^{2}}}+\frac{3}{5} \sqrt{1-\frac{4^{2}}{5^{2}}}\right\} \\\Rightarrow \sin ^{-1}\left\{\frac{16}{25}+\frac{9}{25}\right\} \therefore A=\frac{16}{25}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #673 (ID: 442199)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2008-2009, 2005-2006 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x[/math]এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \quad \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
ধরি[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta=x \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
আবার ধরি, [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \Rightarrow \theta^{\prime}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই,[math]\sin \sin ^{-1} x=x[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #674 (ID: 442200)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2012-2013, 2008-2009, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit), Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (B Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta[/math] হয় [math]\tan \theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]9[/math]
B. [math]7 / 2[/math]
C. [math]3 / 5[/math]
D. [math]4 / 5[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { }\left(\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3\right)+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 4+\tan ^{-1}(-1)=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{4+(-1)}{1-4(-1)}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #680 (ID: 442206)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta=1[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর সাধারণ মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math](2 n-1) \pi / 2[/math]
খ. [math](4 n-1) \pi / 2[/math]
গ. [math](4 n+1) \pi / 2[/math]
ঘ. [math](2 n+1) \pi / 2[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=(4 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} ; \sin \theta=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=(4 n-1) \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\cos \theta=1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=2 n \pi ; \cos \theta=-1[/math]
 হলে [math]\theta=(2 n+1) \pi[/math]


Explanation:

Question #681 (ID: 442207)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2008-2009 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \text { হয়, } \tan \theta=?[/math]

Options:
A. 9
B. 7/2
C. 3/5
D. 4/5


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { Hints: } \tan ^{-1} x+\tan ^{-1} y=\tan ^{-1} \frac{x+y}{1-x y} \\\text { }\left(\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3\right)+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \\\quad \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{2+3}{1-2 \times 3}+\tan ^{-1} 4=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} 4+\tan ^{-1}(-1)=\theta \\\Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{1+(-1)}{1-4(-1)}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{3}{5}=\theta \Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{3}{5}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা: প্রশ্নটির দিকে একটু ভালো করে খেয়াল করলেই বুঝা যায় দুটি করে [math]\tan ^{-1}[/math]
 নিয়ে সূত্র ফেলা যায়। এক্ষেত্রে তাই [math]\tan ^{-1}[/math]
 এর তিনটি মানের মধ্যে প্রথমে দুটো নিয়ে কাজ করে ফলাফলের সাথে তৃতীয়টি নিয়ে একইভাবে সুে ফেলা হয়েছে।

Question #682 (ID: 442208)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\pi / 2[/math] হলে [math]x²+y² =?[/math]

Options:
A. 1
B. 0
C. -1
D. √3/2


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { } \sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\pi / 2 \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y \\\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \sin ^{-1} y \\=\cos \cos ^{-1} \sqrt{1-y^{2}} \Rightarrow x=\sqrt{1-y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #683 (ID: 442209)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan \theta+\cot \theta=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta, 0 \leq \theta<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে θ এর মান কত? (What is the value of θ that satisfies [math]\left.\tan \theta+\cot \theta=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta, 0 \leq \theta<\frac{\pi}{2} ?\right)[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan \theta+\cos =2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math]
বা, [math]\frac{\sin \theta}{\operatorname{Cos} \theta}+\frac{\operatorname{Cos} \theta}{\operatorname{Sin} \theta}=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math]
বা, [math]\frac{\sin \theta}{\cos \theta}+\frac{\cos \theta}{\sin \theta}=2 \operatorname{cosec} \theta[/math]
বা, [math]\frac{1}{\operatorname{Cos} \theta}=2[/math]
বা, [math]\operatorname{Sec} \theta=2[/math]
বা, [math]\theta=\operatorname{Sec}^{1}(2)[/math]
বা, যেহেতু, [math]0 \leq 0<\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Explanation:
এ ধরনের অকেছ ত্রিকোনমিত্তিক অনুপাত ‍গুলোকে ভেঙ্গো সরল করে নেওয়া উচিত। এতে [math]\operatorname{Sin}^{2} \theta+\operatorname{Cos}^{2} \theta=1[/math] সূত্র ব্যবহার করা যায়।

Question #691 (ID: 442176)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2008-2009, 2005-2006 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x[/math]এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]x[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{x}[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \cos ^{-1} x=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \quad \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}[/math]
ধরি[math]\begin{array}{l}\cos ^{-1} x=\theta \Rightarrow \cos \theta=x \\\Rightarrow \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x} \\\Rightarrow \theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}\end{array}[/math]

[Image]
আবার ধরি, [math]\cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{x}=\theta^{\prime}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sin \theta^{\prime}=\frac{x}{1} \Rightarrow \theta^{\prime}=\sin ^{-1} x[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আমরা পাই,[math]\sin \sin ^{-1} x=x[/math]

[Image]


Explanation:

Question #697 (ID: 442182)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology, Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান -

Options:
A. [math]30^{\circ}[/math]
B. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
C. [math]60^{\circ}[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \theta+\cos \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta=1 \Rightarrow \sin 45^{\circ} \sin \theta+\cos 45^{\circ} \cos \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-45^{\circ}\right)=\cos 0 \Rightarrow \theta-45^{\circ}=0 \quad \therefore \theta=45^{\circ} \text {. }[/math]
সমাধান: [math]\sec ^{-1} \frac{x}{y}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y} ; \sin \cos ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{x^{2}-y^{2}}}{y}=\sin \sin ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}=\frac{\sqrt{2 y^{2}-x^{2}}}{y}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #702 (ID: 442187)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{sincot}^{-1} \operatorname{tancos}^{-1}\left(2 x^{2}\right)=1[/math] হলে x এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]1, 1[/math]
B. [math]2, \frac{1}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt{2},-\sqrt{2}[/math]
D. [math]2, -2[/math]
E. [math]\sqrt{2}, \sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
Hints: একটি ত্রিভুজের ভূমি x, লম্ব I হলে অতিভুজ [math]\sqrt{x^{2}+1} ;[/math]
তাহলে [math]\sin ^{-1} x=\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{x}=\cos ^{-1} \frac{x}{\sqrt{x^{2}+1}}[/math]
 লেখা যায়[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { Solve: } \sin \cot ^{-1} \tan ^{-1} 2 x^{2} \\=\sin \cot ^{-1} \tan \tan ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-4 x^{4}}}{2 x^{2}} \\=\sin \cot ^{-1} \frac{\sqrt{1-4 x^{4}}}{2 x^{2}}=\sin \sin ^{-1} 2 x^{2}=2 x^{2} \\\text { যেহেতু } 2 x^{2}=1 \therefore x^{2}=\frac{1}{2} \Rightarrow x= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #703 (ID: 442188)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
A. [math]5[/math]
B. [math]12[/math]
C. [math]7[/math]
D. [math]\text { None of these }[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\tan ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} 3\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow1+\tan ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 4\right)+\sec ^{2}\left(\sec ^{-1} \cdot 3\right)-1=4^{2}+3^{2}=16+9=25[/math]

Question #705 (ID: 442190)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { How many solutions are there for } \sec 4 \theta-\sec 2 \theta=2 ;\left[0^{\circ}<\theta<180^{\circ}\right][/math]

Options:
A. [math]3[/math]
B. [math]5[/math]
C. [math]6[/math]
D. [math]9[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{\cos 4 \theta}-\frac{1}{\cos 2 \theta}=2 \Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta-\cos 4 \theta=2 \cos 4 \theta \cos 2 \theta[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos 2 \theta-\cos 4 \theta=\cos 6 \theta+\cos 2 \theta \Rightarrow \cos 6 \theta+\cos 4 \theta=0 \Rightarrow 2 \cos 5 \theta \cos \theta=0 \\\Rightarrow \cos 5 \theta=0 \Rightarrow 5 \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \theta=(2 n+1) \frac{\pi}{10}=\frac{\pi}{2}, \frac{3 \pi}{2}, \frac{7 \pi}{10}, \frac{9 \pi}{10} \text { or }, \cos \theta=0 \therefore \theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #706 (ID: 442191)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Islamic University of Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\text { Evaluate: } \tan ^{-1} \frac{m}{n}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{m-n}{m+n} \text {. }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{9}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{-1} \frac{m}{n}-\tan ^{-1} \frac{m-n}{m+n}=\tan ^{-1}\left[\frac{\frac{m}{n}-\frac{m-n}{m+n}}{1+\frac{m}{n} \cdot\left(\frac{m-n}{m+n}\right)}\right][/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\tan ^{-1}\left[\frac{m^{2}+m n-m n+n^{2}}{n(m+n)+m(m-n)}\right]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{m^{2}+n^{2}}{m^{2}+n^{2}}\right)=\tan ^{-1} 1=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #707 (ID: 442192)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011, 2006-2007 | Boards:  | Tags: Chattogram Veterinary and Animal Sciences University, Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]-5[/math]
B. [math]5[/math]
C. [math]-4[/math]
D. [math]4[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sec ^{2}\left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)=1+\left\{\tan \left(\tan ^{-1} 2\right)\right\}^{2}=1+(2)^{2}=1+4=5[/math]

Question:
[math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

A. [math]\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1-x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sin \left(2 \tan ^{-1} x\right)=\sin \left(\sin ^{-1} \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}\right)=\frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]

Question #709 (ID: 442194)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2008-2009 | Boards:  | Tags: Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sec \theta=-2[/math] এবং [math]\frac{\pi}{2}<\theta<\pi ; \theta[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{3 x}{4}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{5 x}{6}[/math]
D. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sec \theta=-2 \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\cos \theta}=-2 \Rightarrow \cos \theta=-\frac{1}{2} \Rightarrow \theta=\cos ^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\right)=120^{\circ} \therefore \theta=\frac{2 \mathrm{~s}}{3}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #710 (ID: 442195)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2008-2009 | Boards:  | Tags: Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{2} x+\sec ^{2} x=3[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান -

Options:
A. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{1}{4} \pi[/math]
B. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
D. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{1}{4} \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\tan ^{2} x+\sec ^{2} x=3 \Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x+1+\tan ^{2} x=3 \Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} x+1=3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \tan ^{2} x=1 \Rightarrow \tan x=\pm 1 \Rightarrow \tan x=\tan \left(\pm \frac{\pi}{4}\right) \therefore x=n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #711 (ID: 442196)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2019-2020, 2017-2018, 2017-2018, 2016-2017, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (A Unit, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math] হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]1x+y=1[/math]
ঘ. [math]x-y=1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sin ^{-1} x+\sin ^{-1} y=\frac{\pi}{2} \Rightarrow \sin ^{-1} x=\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow x=\sin \left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x=\cos \left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\cos ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \Rightarrow x^{2}=1-\sin ^{2}\left(\sin ^{-1} y\right) \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=1-y^{2} \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #712 (ID: 442197)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2006-2007, 2003-2004, 2022-2023, 2008-2009, 2005-2006 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan ^{-1} 1+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\pi[/math]
D. [math]2\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\therefore x y+y z+z x=1 \times 2+2 \times 3+3 \times 1=11>1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \tan ^{-1} 1+\tan ^{-1} 2+\tan ^{-1} 3 \\=\pi+\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1+2+3-1 \times 2 \times 3}{1-1 \times 2-2 \times 3-3 \times 1}\right)=\pi+\tan ^{-1}(0)=\pi+0=\pi\end{array}[/math]

Question #713 (ID: 442198)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2018-2019, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2014-2015, 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2012-2013, 2010-2011, 2007-2008, 2004-2005, 2003-2004 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (A Unit), Mymensingh Board, Begum Rokeya University, Rangpur (A Unit), Bangamata Sheikh Fojilatunnesa Mujib Science and Technology University, University of Chittagong (Unit A), University of Barishal, Mymensingh Board, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2}[/math] হলে, [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi[/math]
B. [math](2 n+1) \pi[/math]
C. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math](2 n-1) \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\cos \theta+\sin \theta=\sqrt{2} \Rightarrow \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \cos \theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} \sin \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \cos \theta \cos \frac{\pi}{4}+\sin \theta \sin \frac{\pi}{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow \cos \left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1 \Rightarrow \theta-\frac{\pi}{4}=2 \mathrm{n} \pi \therefore \theta=2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{4}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #714 (ID: 442174)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2021-2022, 2013-2014, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2016-2017, 2013-2014, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Bangladesh University of Textiles, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology, Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1[/math] সমীকরণে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{8}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]n \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]2 n \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\tan 2 \theta \tan \theta=1 \Rightarrow \frac{2 \tan \theta}{1-\tan ^{2} \theta} \times \tan \theta=1[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 2 \tan ^{2} \theta=1-\tan ^{2} \theta \Rightarrow 3 \tan ^{2} \theta=1 \\\Rightarrow\tan \theta= \pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\tan \left( \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\right) \therefore\theta=\mathrm{n} \pi \pm \frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #718 (ID: 792777)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: 
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\sec\theta = -2[/math] এবং [math]{\pi \over 2} &lt; \theta &lt; \pi[/math] হয়, তবে [math]\theta[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. <p><span class="math-tex">\(-{2\pi \over 3}\)</span></p>

খ. <p><span class="math-tex">\(2\pi \over 3\)</span></p>

গ. <p><span class="math-tex">\(-\pi\)</span></p>
